eṅkum kalavaram mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭa attaruṇattil, vācavatattiyiṉ niṉivu varappeṟṟa piraccōtaṉaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭu, ‘yātu ceyvatu’ eṉat tikittāṉ. uṭaṉē utayaṇaṉip paṟṟiya karuttu eḻuntatu. taṉ māṇākkiyākiya tattiyik kāppatu utayaṇaṉ kaṭaṉeṉac cilar ﻿ mūlam avaṉukkuc ceyti colli aṉuppiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ. piraccōtaṉaṉ taṉakku aṉuppiya ceyti kēṭṭu, kumpiṭappōṉa teyvam kuṟukkē vantateṉa makiḻntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattiyik kippaṟṟat tāṉē avaṉ aṅkē niṟkiṟāṉ? takka ceytiyi maṉṉaṉum aṉuppiviṭṭāṉ. iṉic caṟṟaḷavu taṭiyum irukka niyāyamilli. ‘vāṉaveḷi iṭintu viḻiṉum vīḻka, maṉṉar tattiyip paṟṟic ciṟitum kalaṅka vēṇṭā. avaḷik kāppatu eṉ kaṭaṉ’ eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟi aṉuppi viṭṭut tatti nīrāṭum tuṟi nōkkip pattirāpatiyi virivākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

tatti nīrāṭum tuṟiyi aṭinta utayaṇaṉ, “iṉi ivaḷ iṅkē ciṟitu nēramum taṅkutal naṉṟaṉṟu. eṉṉuṭaṉ varuka! piṭiyēṟṟic ceṉṟu tattiyik kāppatu eṉ kaṭaṉeṉa āṇiyiṭṭirukkiṟār aracar” eṉṟu avaḷ āyattāri nōkkik kūṟiṉāṉ. atik kēṭṭa vācavatatti nāṇamum makiḻcciyum kalantu kāl nilaṅ kiḷippak kātaluṇarvu mēliṭat talikuṉintu niṉṟāḷ. niṟikkum nāṇattiṟkum avaḷuḷḷē oru pūcal naṭanta vaṇṇamiruntatu. utayaṇaṉi niṉintālō neñcam urukiyatu.

āṉāl avaṉōṭu eppaṭi yāṉiyil ēṟip pōvateṉa nāṇam taṭuttatu. kāñcaṉamāli eṉṟa tōḻi tattiyit taṉ kikaḷāl tāṅkiya vaṇṇam piṭiyiṉ mītu ēṟṟa arukē aḻittu vantaṉaḷ. tattiyik kikoṭuttu yāṉi mēlē tūkkip piṭariyiṉ mītu ēṟṟik koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. oruvari oruvar kippaṟṟiyapōtu avarkaḷukkuḷ ētō puriya muṭiyāta iṉpa mayakkam pukuntu viḷiyāṭiyatu. pūṅkoṭi oṉṟi mēlē tūkkuvatu pōṉṟa poliviyum meṉmiyiyum utayaṇaṉ uṇarntāṉ. maṉattilē kōyilkoṇṭa kātalaṉiṉ karaṅkaḷ mēlē eṭuttaṇippatit tatti uṇarntāḷ. ﻿piṭiyāṉi naṭantatu. utayaṇaṉ ūṟētumiṉṟi veṟṟiyuṟṟa perumitam koṇṭāṉ.

tattikkut tuṇiyākak kāñcaṉa māliyum piṭiyil ēṟikkoṇṭāḷ. appōtu vayantakaṉ taṉkiyil kōṭapati eṉṟa yāḻuṭaṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. tatti utayaṇaṉ vaṭivaḻaki nōkki viyanta vaṇṇam iruntāḷ. appōtu tattiyik kaṇṭu pēca varupavaḷ pōlac cāṅkiyat tāy aṅkē vantāḷ. yūkiyiṉ aṅka aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi avaḷ pāl vivarittu, mēlē nikaḻa vēṇṭiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷi virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷa oliyoṉṟi yūkiyiṭam aḷikkumāṟu avaḷiṭam utayaṇaṉ tantāṉ. itu mika maṟimukamāka naṭantatu. piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi oliyip piṭiyiṉ mēliruntu kīḻē naḻuvaviṭṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ cāṅkiyat tāyiṭam ati eṭuttuc cērppittiāṉ. cāṅkiyat tāy yūkikkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷum cūḻcciyum aṭaṅkiya oliyik koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṟintāḷ. cellumpōtu tattiyiṭam kaṇkaḷ nīr perukka avaḷ viṭi peṟṟuk koṇṭakāṭci maṟakka muṭiyātatu. vayantakaṉum ēṟikkoṇṭapiṉ piṭiyit taṉnāṭu cellum peruvaḻi nōkki virivāka naṭattiṉaṉ utayaṇaṉ. ituvari oṉṟum puriyāmal aracaṉāṇiyāl tattiyi utayaṇaṉ kāttaṟkākap piṭiyēṟṟukiṟāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇiyirunta piraccōtaṉaṉiṉ vīrar ippōtu tikippuṭaṉ tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar. yūkiyiṉ vīrarkaḷukkum utayaṇaṉ pōkkit taṭukka muṉvanta ucciyiṉi vīrarkaḷukkum kikalappu ēṟpaṭṭatu. pōrākavum māṟi nikaḻntatu. piṭiyiṉ mēl tatti, kāñcaṉamāli, vayantakaṉ ivarkaḷuṭaṉ viriviṟ ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉiṉ muṉṉē varākaṉeṉṟa piraccōtaṉaṉuṭiya vīraṉ etirppaṭṭāṉ. av vīraṉ taṉmēl cantēkamuṟṟu ētēṉum ūṟu ceytalum kūṭum eṉṟeṇṇi utayaṇaṉ avaṉi nōkki, “varāka! nammic cuṟṟip pōriṭṭuk kalakam viḷivippavarkaḷ māṟuvēṭattiluḷḷa ﻿tiruṭarkaḷākavum irukkalām. etaṟkum nī eṉṉuṭaṉ āyuta pāṇiyākap piṭiyiṉmēl varuvatu nallatu” eṉṟāṉ. utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi uṇmi eṉṟu nampiya varākaṉ piṭi mītēṟuvataṟkākat taṉ villiyum ampiyum utayaṇaṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu neruṅkiṉāṉ. villum ampum taṉ kikku varappeṟṟa utayaṇaṉ, ‘iṉi ivaṉāṟ payamilli!’ eṉṟu ēṟavanta varākaṉukku iṭaṅkoṭāmaṟ piṭiyi vēkamākac celuttik koṇṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ. ēṟutaṟku neruṅki iṭaṟivīḻttappaṭṭa varākaṉum vīrarkaḷ cilarum utayaṇaṉ cūḻcciyip purintukoṇṭaṉar. avantiyar vēntaṉākiya piraccōtaṉaṉ tiru muṉṉarppōy yātu kūṟuvatu eṉṟañci mayaṅkiṉar. irupaṭikkum iṭiyē pōr naṭantatu. koṟṟavaṉ āṇi tappiya koṭumikku mikavum veruviya avanti vīrar oṉṟukūṭi, utayaṇaṉ cellum piṭiyip piṉpaṟṟi ōṭiṉar. avvāṟu oṭiṉavarkaḷi vattavanāṭṭu vittaka vīrar, vāḷ koṇṭu etirttaṉar. ‘vattavaṉ vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ tatti eṉṟa ceytiyi maṉṉaṉ aṟiyiṉ eṉ ceyvāṉō?’ eṉa naṭuṅkiya vīrar, ip pōril uyirē pōyiṉum kavali illi eṉat tuṇintu muṉ vantaṉar. muṉṉēṟpāṭṭuṭaṉ aṅkaṅkē oḷintirunta yūkiyiṉ vīrarkaḷ ainnūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭavar veḷivantu piraccōtaṉaṉ āṭkaḷōṭu pōriṭṭaṉar. irutarappiṉarukkum iṭiyē kaṭumpōr nikaḻntatu. yūkiyiṉ paṭiyiṉar vīciya vāḷkaḷil pakivar ciraṅkaḷ paṉaṅkaṉik kuliyiliruntu utirvatupōla utirntaṉa. kuruti veḷḷam āṟeṉap parantatu. appōtu yūki taṉ vāḷuṭaṉ aṅkē tōṉṟi utayaṇaṉic cantittāṉ. piṭimītu virivākac ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉ piṭiyi niṟutti yūkiyi varavēṟṟāṉ. utayaṇaṉukku iṟutivari veṟṟiyi aḷikkumāṟu avaṉ ēṟik koṇṭirunta yāṉiyākiya pattirāpatiyi vāḻttiṉāṉ yūki. naṇparkaḷ mēlē naṭakka vēṇṭiya ﻿ tiṭṭaṅkaḷi oruvarukkoruvar aṟivittuk koṇṭaṉar. cāṅkiyat tāyip paṟṟi yūkikku virivākak kūṟi, avaḷi mēlnaṭakkum viṉikaḷil nampip payaṉ koḷḷalām eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. avaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ yāvum aṭaṅkiya ōli yoṉṟiyum utayaṇaṉiṭamiruntu yūki peṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. piṉṉē turatti varum paṭi neruṅkiviṭavē yūkiyiṭam viṭipeṟṟup piṭiyi virivākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. yūki makkaḷ kūṭṭattil kalantu maṟintāṉ. muṉṉar ēmāṟṟappaṭṭa varākaṉ ippōtu utayaṇaṉip piṉtoṭarnta paṭiyiṉar muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟāṉ. pōkiṉṟa pōkkil piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉukkuc cila ceyti kūṟic celkiṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam ituvari nāṉ ciṟippaṭ ṭiruntēṉ. iḻivu karutāta eṉṉit taṉ makaṭku yāḻ kaṟpikkac ceytāṉ. kaṟpittēṉ. ivaḷmēṟ kātal koḷḷa nērntatu. iṉṟu avaṉ vāyālēyē ivaḷik kākkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ivaḷi eṉ nāṭu koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇakkak karutiṉēṉ. am maṉṉaṉukku eṉ vaṇakkattik kūṟuka” eṉṟu ivvāṟu varākaṉukkuk kēṭkumpaṭi urittu viṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. varākaṉukku utayaṇaṉāṟ kūṟappeṟṟa im maṟumoḻi kēṭṭut tatti caṟṟē ciṉaṅ koṇṭāḷ, kāñcaṉamāliyik ‘kīḻē iṟaṅkiviṭuka’ eṉṉum kuṟipput tōṉṟa nōkkiṉāḷ. ak kuṟippi aṟintu koṇṭa kāñcaṉamāli “koṭiyūcalil tōḻiyar kikōttāṭiṉum kuravik kūttu āṭiṉum em talivi naṭuṅkuvaḷ. cūṟāvaḷik kāṟṟeṉac cuḻaṉṟu ōṭum piṭiyāl avaḷukku acca muṇṭākiṟatu. piṉ toṭarum eṅkaḷ paṭiyi oru poruṭṭāka matiyāmal nīvir piṭiyi ittuṇi viriviṟ celuttuvatu eṉ karutiyō? eṅkaḷ maṉam payappaṭukiṟatu” eṉa vaṇaṅkiya kiyuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉik kēṭṭāḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya cantēkam utayaṇaṉukkum purintatu.



kāñcaṉamāliyiṉ viṉāvukku ēṟṟa viṭi kūṟit tēṟṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “aracaṉ tattiyik kākkumāṟu eṉṉip paṇitta ceytiyi aṟiyātu inta vīrarkaḷ cantēkamuṟṟup pōrukku varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷi veṉṟu meṟcellutal eṉakku ariya viṉiyaṉṟu. eṉavē nīṅkaḷ iruvarum ciṟitum añcal vēṇṭā” eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ivvāṟu kūṟiya moḻikaḷik kāñcaṉi tattikku āyattic cērnta koṭṭantāṅkiya paṇippeṇkaḷ, aṭippi makaḷir, cuvarimakaḷir, paṇiceykūṉar, tōḻiyar, ceviliyat tāyar, cañcukimutiyar mutaliyār yātu ceyvateṉṟaṟiyātu alamaral eytip pulampiṉar. nīrāṭa vanta makkaḷ kūṭṭam tuyarāṭit tuṉpattil āḻntu koṇṭiruntatu. iḵtu ivvāṟu irukka utayaṇaṉiṭam ōli peṟṟuc ceṉṟa cāṅkiyat tāy periya paṭip parappiṉ naṭuvē māṟu vēṭattuṭaṉ tirikiṉṟa yūkiyi avaṉ uṭal aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāṟ kaṇṭu aṟintāḷ. taṉṉi aṟintu niṟkum caṅkiyat tāyik kūrntu nōkkiya yūki utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyirunta vivaraṅkaḷip paṟṟi muṭivu ceyvataṟkāka arukiṉ irunta orukuyavaṉ maṉiyil maṟintu iruvarum ālōcittaṉar. ālōcaṉi muṭintapiṉ mīṇṭum tām cantikkum kālatti oruvarukkoruvar aṟivittuk koṇṭu pirintu ceṉṟaṉar.





14. ciṉamum cintaṉiyum

tattiyi utayaṇaṉ taṉ nāṭu nōkkip piṭimēl koṇṭu celvati aṟintum atip piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam kūṟa añciṉar, kāvalvīrar. kāvalvīrar palarum ivvāṟu añci eṉ ceyvateṉṟu aṟiyāmal tikittiruntapōtu villum ampum utayaṇaṉiṭam paṟikoṭuttuviṭṭu avaṉiṭamiruntu maṉṉaṉukkuc ceyti kēṭṭu vanta varākaṉ paṭamāṭak kōvilil irunta aracaṉik kāṇak kaṭuki ōṭiṉaṉ. “aracar perumāṉik kaṇṭu cila avacarac ceytikaḷik kūṟavēṇṭum. camayamaṟintu varuka” eṉṟu vāyiṟ kāvalaṉiṭam kūṟi yaṉuppiviṭṭu, ōṭivanta iḷippāl mēl mūccu vāṅka vāyiṟkaṭiyil niṉṟāṉ varākaṉ. kāvalaṉ uḷḷē ceṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ paṭamāṭak kōviluḷḷē appōtu oru nimittikaṉuṭaṉ pēcik koṇṭiruntāṉ. ‘kāṟṟum maḻiyumāka eḻunta nili ūrukku mutalil mikka tuyarattiyum piṉṉar niṉakku iṉpattiyum tarum’ eṉṟu nimittikaṉ aracaṉukkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu kuṟippākak kūṟiya nimittikaṉiṭam piracacōtaṉaṉ ikkūṟṟit taṉakku naṉṟāka viḷakki urikkumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāṉ.

appōtu vāyiṟkāvalaṉ paraparappum avacaramum tōṉṟa vaṇaṅkiyavāṟu uḷḷē vantāṉ. “vantavaṉ mukattil tōṉṟum paraparappik kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ ‘vanta kāriyam yātu?” eṉa avaṉiṭam viṉaviṉāṉ. vāyiṟ kāvalaṉ paṇinta kuralil, “vācavatattikkuk kāvalvīrarāka uḷḷavarkaḷil oruvaṉākiya varākaṉ vāyalilē niṟkiṟāṉ. avaṉ taṅkaḷiṭam uṭaṉ kūṟattakka ceytikaḷ cila uḷavām” eṉṟāṉ. araciyal uṇmikaḷi antaraṅkamāka ārāyum pōtum makaḷākiya tattiyip paṟṟiya ceyti eṉṟāl kēṭṭuviṭṭuttāṉ maṟuvēli pārppāṉ piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ. tattiyiṉiṭam avaṉukku uḷḷa aṉpu attakiyatu. ākavē nimittikaṉōṭu uriyāṭik koṇṭiruntālum, “varākaṉi uṭaṉē varaccol?” eṉṟukūṟik kāvalaṉi aṉuppiṉāṉ. kāvalaṉ, aracaṉukkup pallāṇṭu kūṟi vāḻttum vaṇakkamum aḷittuc ceṉṟāṉ. kāvalaṉ ceṉṟa ciṟitu nērattiṟkuḷ varākaṉ naṭukkaṅkalanta accamum paraparappum uḷḷavaṉāy uḷḷē vantāṉ. vantavaṉ aracaṉukku ēḻukōl elli taḷḷi mariyātiyāka niṉṟu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ mukattōṟṟattiyum naṭukkattiyum kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ, ivaṉ kūṟa vantirukkum ceyti tuyaram viḷikkak kūṭiyatāy irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇi avaṉit taṉakku mikappakkattil irukōl elliyuḷ aḻittāṉ. varākaṉ neruṅki vantatum, “vantatu yātu kūṟavō? ataṉi virivil kūṟu” eṉṟu āṇi piṟantatu.

tariyil muṭitōya maṉṉaṉi vaṇaṅki eḻunta varākaṉ, tayaṅkit tayaṅki niṉṟāṉē oḻiya vāytiṟantu naṭantatik kūṟa añciṉāṉ. “neñcil añcātu nikaḻntatik kūṟu” eṉṟu iṭimuḻakkam pōṉṟa kuralil mīṇṭum āṇiyiṭṭāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. ippōtu varākaṉukkuc ciṟitu tuṇivu piṟantatu. aracaṉ taṉ uyirukku apayamaḷippāṉ eṉṟa nampikkiyum tōṉṟiyatu.

nikaḻntavaṟṟi ovvoṉṟākak kūṟiṉāṉ: “utayaṇaṉ tattiyuṭaṉ piṭiyēṟit taṉ nāṭu nōkkic celkiṟāṉ. piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟa nam paṭi māṟṟār paṭiyāl aḻintu taṭi yuṟṟatu. yāṉ virintu piṭiyip piṉpaṟṟi neruṅkiṉēṉ. ‘tattiyi eṉpāl aṭikkalam aḷitta uṅkaḷ vēntar pirāṉukku eṉ vaṇakkattik kūṟuka’ eṉṟu kikūppi viṭṭuk kāṟṟeṉa virivuṭaṉ piṭiyic celutti maṟintāṉ utayaṇaṉ” eṉṟu varākaṉ kūṟiyatum piraccōtaṉaṉuṭiya virinta malarviḻikaḷ civantaṉa. puruvaṅkaḷ nerintaṉa. mukattil ciṉatti eḻuntu paravip paṭarvatu terintatu. utayaṇaṉ turōkam ceytuviṭṭāṉ eṉpatu avaṉukku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.

“utayaṇaṉi etirttu uṭaṉē nampaṭi puṟappaṭaṭṭum eḷḷi viḷiyāṭukiṟatupōlum iḷami. nalla paṇpuḷḷavaṉ eṉ nampiṉēṉ. nampikkiyic citittu viṭṭāṉ vattavar kōṉ. nāṉ yāreṉa aṟiyāṉpōlum. cellaṭṭum nam paṭi. antac ciṟu maṉṉaṉip piṭittu iḻuttu vāruṅkaḷ.” ivvāṟu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu muḻaṅkiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. ciṉam eṉpatu vēkamākap paṟṟip paravum kāṭṭut tīyip pōṉṟatu. paṇpaṭṭa uḷḷam paṭittavarkaḷkūṭa ataṟku mikavirivil uṭpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ mikacciṟanta nākarikap paṇpuṭiyavaṉāka iruntum oru noṭiyil taṉṉi iḻantu ciṉattiṟku aṭimippaṭṭu viṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ niliyiyum appōtu avaṉukku irunta ciṉattiṉāl ēṟpaṭa irukkum viḷivukaḷiyum naṉku aṟintukoṇṭa avaṉuṭiya mutaṉ mantiri cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ, amitiyākac cila aṟivurikaḷik kūṟiṉāṉ. “utayaṇaṉ ceytatu eṉṉavō tavaṟutāṉ! ataṟkāka avaṉic ciṟi ceytalum kiḷiñariṭamiruntu pirittalum periya kāriyamalla. avikaḷi nī virumpiṉ niṉṉāl eḷitiṟ ceyya muṭiyum. oṉṟu nī cintikka vēṇṭum. ārāyntu pārttāl vācavatattikku utayaṇaṉ evvitattilum kuṟintavaṉ ākāṉ. kulam, kuṇam, naṭpu, nilam mutaliyavaṟṟālum avaṉ niṉṉi yotta aracaṉē. avaṉ tattiyik koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇantu koḷvatil tavaṟu oṉṟumilli. ūḻviṉi avvāṟu amiyum pōtu ataṉip paḻippatilum payaṉ illi. eṉavē ciṉakkoṇṭu paṭiyeḻupputal ciṟanta vaḻi aṉṟu. maṉattil paki irukkumāṉāl ippōtu ati veḷikkāṭṭal vēṇṭā. piṉpu vēṟu vaḻiyākat tattiyuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉi mīṭka muyala vēṇṭum. uṭaṉaṭiyāka ivvāṟu pōr mēṟkoḷvatu aṟivukku ēṟṟatu aṉṟu” eṉṟu virivākavum karuttuḷḷaṭaṅkiyatumākac cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ kūṟiya āṟutal piraccōtaṉaṉ maṉattil aḻuttamākap paṭintu viṭṭatu. amiccaṉ kūṟiya āṟutalāl maṉantēṟiya piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, taṉ kōpperuntēvikku ic ceyti maṟṟavarkaḷāl aṟivikkappaṭumuṉ pakkuvamākat tāṉē ceṉṟu aṟivikka virintāṉ.

ivvaḷavum natikkariyil aracaṉukkeṉa amikkappaṭṭirunta paṭamāṭak kōvilil naṭantaṉa. tēviyik kāṇappōkumuṉ muracaṟiyum vaḷḷuvaṉik kūppiṭac ceytu maḻippoḻutākavum paṉiporuntiyatāyum uḷḷa im māliyil tīrttat tuṟiyiliruntu nakar cella vēṇṭāveṉa makkaḷ yāvarkkum muracaṟittu aṟivikkavum, kāliyil nakar pōkavēṇṭumeṉṟu paṇikkavum ceyyumāṟu kūṟic ceṉṟāṉ. ceṉṟavaṉ nērē tirumā tēviyiṉ ciṅkārap paḷḷiyaṟiyuḷ nuḻintāṉ. nīrt tuṟiyil aracaṉ viṭutikku arukē amikkappaṭṭirunta acciṅkāra māḷiki palavaki aṇikaḷuṅkoṇṭu pārppōrkkuk kaviṉ koṭuttu viḷaṅkiyatu. maṉṉaṉ varavaṟintu etirkoḷḷa vanta peruntēviyiṉ maṉamō, kalavaramum tiruptiyum kalantu oru niliyil nillāmal cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu. utayaṇaṉē kāppāṟṟuvataṉāl, taṉ makaḷ tattikkut tītu nērātu eṉṟa tirupti iruntatu. āṉāl utayaṇaṉ tattiyit taṉ nāṭu koṇṭu ēkuvāṉ eṉṟa niṉivē kaṉavil kūṭat tēvikku illi.

palavita eṇṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tēvi maṉṉaṉi varavēṟṟāḷ. uṇavu muṭintapiṉ vanta ceytiyi virivākak kūṟak karutiṉāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ. aṉṟu uṇavē vēṇṭiyirukkavilli maṉṉaṉukku. iruntālum tāṉ uṇṇa maṟuttāl tirumā tēvi aiyuṟa nērum eṉpataṟkāka ētō oruvāṟu uṇavi muṭittāṉ. iruvarum paḷḷi maṇṭapattil vantu amarntaṉar. cintaṉiyip poṟutta variyil iruvarukkum oṉṟum puriyāta kalavaramoṉṟu uḷḷē kuḻampik koṇṭiruntatu. tāṉ colla vantatip pakkuvamiṉṟi eṭutta eṭuppil, ‘utayaṇaṉ tattiyik koṇṭu ōṭiviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāl tēvikku eṉṉa nērumō eṉṟu añciya maṉṉaṉ mikavum nayamāṉa oru vaḻiyip piṉpaṟṟi ati avaḷukkuk kūṟa muṭivu ceytāṉ. “tattiyi utayaṇaṉ maṇappatu paṟṟit tēviyiṉ karuttu yātu?” eṉa muṉpiṉ toṭarpillāta oru kēḷviyit tiṭīreṉa avaḷiṭam kēṭṭāṉ aracaṉ. intak kēḷviyiṉāl kōpperuntēviyiṉ tiruvuḷak kuṟippi aṟiya virumpiya piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, avaḷ maṟumoḻi kūṟāmaliruppatu kaṇṭu iṉṉum teḷivāka, “tattikku yāḻ kaṟpitta peruntakiyaṉ utayaṇaṉukkē avaḷi maṇam ceytu koṭuttu avaṉ nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppalām eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. nī yātu karutiyirukkiṟāy? itu niṉakku makiḻcciyit tarum ceytitāṉā?” eṉṟu mīṇṭum kēṭṭāṉ. ik kēḷvi mūlamē avaḷ viruppatti aṟiyat tiṭṭamiṭṭiruntāṉ avaṉ. tēvi itu kēṭṭu mukamalarntāḷ. avaḷ uṭaṉpāṭu aracaṉukku am malarcciyiṉālēyē pulaṉāyiṟṟu.

“yāṉiyi matamaṭakki vanta aṉṟu, mutaṉ mutalāka avaṉik kaṇṭapōtē, avaṉ iḷamipolivu eṉ maṉattiṟ patintuviṭṭatu. aṉṟē avaṉ tattikku ēṟṟa koḻunaṉeṉak karutiṉēṉ yāṉ. kuṟṟamaṟṟa aracar kuṭiyilē tōṉṟiya kōk kumaraṉ ātaliṉ, avaṉukkut tattiyi ēṟkum takutiyilum kuṟipāṭilli” eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāḷ kōpperuntēvi. avaḷ maṉam utayaṇaṉ tattikkuriyavaṉāvatit taṭukkum niliyil illi. makiḻavē ceykiṟatu eṉṟu teḷintapiṉ piraccōtaṉaṉ aṉṟu naṭantatik kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. “tatti vattava kumaraṉoṭu avaṉ nāṭu ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷ” eṉṟu maṉṉaṉ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ tirumātēviyiṉ cevikaḷilē neruppeṉa nuḻintaṉa. peṇṇip pirinta peṟṟavaḷ perun tuyaraṅkoṇṭāḷ. āvi patittu aḻutāḷ. āṟākat tuyaram avalamāka urukkoḷḷa araṟṟiṉāḷ. tattiyiṉ pirivu avaḷi vāṭṭiyatu.

acuṇam eṉṟa vilaṅku iciyik kēṭṭu iṉpuṟum; paṟiyoli kēṭṭut tuṉpuṟum. tattiyi utayaṇaṉukku maṇa muṭikkalām eṉṟa ceyti kēṭṭiṉpuṟṟa tēvi, aṭuttu avaḷ vattava kumaraṉuṭaṉ avaṉ nāṭu ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉpatu kēṭṭu aḷaviṭa iyalāta tuyaram koṇṭāḷ. kūṟṟuvaṉiṉ koṭumpācattāl tāṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pōṉṟa payaṅkarap pirivuṇarcci tattiyiṉ pirivuc ceyti kēṭṭapōtu avaḷukku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. peṟṟavaḷ avvāṟu tuyarkoṇṭatu peruviyappukkuriyatu allavē? āyiṉum piraccōtaṉaṉ makaḷip pirinta maṉat tuyaram tīra avaḷit tēṟṟiṉāṉ.

“tām vēṇṭum kātaṟ koḻunaṉōṭu cellātu peṟṟōriṭattut taṅkiyirukkum makaḷir ulakil evviṭattum ilar. kaṭalil piṟanta muttu aṇivōrkku allāmal kaṭalukku orupōtum payaṉpaṭuvatu illi. tattiyum namakku appaṭippaṭṭa urimi maṭṭum uṭiyavaḷtāṉē? itaṟkāka nī mikavuṅ kavalutal takutiyuṭiya ceyal aṉṟu” eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiya tēṟṟurikaḷ tēvikku oruvāṟu āṟutal aḷittaṉa. tuyar caṟṟē vilakiyatu.

vāṉmati iḻanta mīṉiṉam pōla, vācavatatti illāta tuyaram peṟṟavaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟu, antap perunakar muḻuvatiyum paṟṟi vāṭṭiyatu. nīrāṭum tuṟiyil kāliyil irunta āravāram ippōtu pōṉa iṭam teriya villi. orē cūṉiya amiti kuṭikoṇṭiruntatu. tūrattē aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka nakaril terinta pukip paṭalaṅkaḷ mēkattōṭu kalantu koṇṭiruntaṉa. kāṟṟum kuḷirum cūḻap peytukoṇṭirunta ciṟu maḻikūṭa - vāṉ makaḷ, tattiyiṉ pirivuttuyar poṟātu kaṇṇir perukkuvatu pōliruntatu. mappum mantāramumākak kāṇappaṭṭa vāṉa araṅkiṉ vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa karunīlap peruveḷi eṅkum tuyar niṟintu tōṉṟiyatu, anta nakarukku. maṉṉaṉum tirumātēviyumkūṭa oruvāṟu tattam tuyara veḷḷatti maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.

nīrāṭṭu viḻā iṉpamayamāka ārampittut tuṉpamayamāka muṭintuviṭṭatu. mutalil iṉpam. naṭuvum iṟutiyum tuṉpam.





﻿15. paki naṭuvē payaṇam

yūkiyiṭam viṭi peṟṟukkoṇṭu, piṉ toṭarukiṉṟa varākaṉ mutaliyōr aṭaṅkiya paṭikkut tappic ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉ vēkamāka mēlē pōka muṭintālum annili nīṭikkavilli. virivil vēṟōr perumpaṭi taṉṉit turattip piṉpaṟṟuvati avaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. utayaṇaṉiṭamiruntu ceyti peṟṟuc ceṉṟa varākaṉ ati aracaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu mēlē vivarikkap peṟṟatu. avaṉ kūṟiyatu kēṭṭa piraccōtaṉaṉ, ciṉaṅkoṇṭatum cālaṅkāyaṇaṉ avaṉi aṭakkiyatum kaṇṭōm. piraccōtaṉaṉ, cālaṅkāyaṉaṉ uriyil āṟutal peṟuvataṟkum muṉpu ceyta ciṉak kalavarattik kaṇṭa varākaṉ, palar kuḻumiya oru perum paṭiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭum utayaṇaṉit turattiṉāṉ. atutāṉ utayaṇaṉ ippōtu kaṇṭapaṭi. ivvāṟu tattiyuṭaṉē oruṅkamarntu cellum anta iṉpap payaṇamum paki cūḻnta payaṇamākiyatu. noṭikku noṭi avaṉ ceṉṟa piṭiyiṉ vēkam atikamākiyatu. ati evvaḷavu turitamākac celutta muṭiyumō avvaḷavu turitamākac celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tērum yāṉiyumākat tiraṇṭa vempaṭi taṉṉi mikamika neruṅki varuvati utayaṇaṉ uṇarntāṉ. anta nērattil camayattiṟkiṭitta cañcīvipōla aṅkē taṉittaṉiyē maṟintirunta yūkiyiṉ paṭivīrar veḷippaṭṭu utayaṇaṉit turatti varum paṭiyi etirttaṉar. utayaṇaṉ itaṟkāka yūkiyi maṉamāṟa vāḻttiṉāṉ. yūkiyiṉ āṭkaḷukku varukiṟa paṭivīrar kaḷippataṟkuḷ tāṉ muṉṉēṟip pōyviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu utayaṇaṉ karuttāyiruntatu. ataṉāltāṉ avaṉ yāṉiyi vēkamāka naṭattiṉāṉ. vantapaṭi yūkiyiṉ vīrarāl etirkkappaṭṭup puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭiyatu. utayaṇaṉ piṭi uñcēṉi nakarukku appāl iraṇṭu kātaturam kaṭantu viṭṭatu. anta nērattil katiravaṉ mella mali mukaṭṭil mēṟkē maṟintu koṇṭiruntāṉ. iruḷ toṭaṅkiyatu. paki naṭuvē payaṇam naṭattukiṉṟa utayaṇaṉi maṟṟavarkaḷ kāṇiṉ tuṉpam nērum eṉṟu karutiyavaṉpōla virivil iruḷ varuvataṟkākak kuṭakaṭal kuḷittuk kuṭamali maṟintāṉ katiravaṉ.

cuṟṟip pakivar irupparō eṉṟa aiyam iruppiṉum iruḷum oḷiyum kalantu mayaṅkum anta māli nēram mikavum irammiyamāka iruntatu. maruta nilaṅkaḷ cūḻnta vaḻiyiṉ aḻakil taṅkaḷiyē maṟantu iṉpaveḷḷattil mitappatu pōṉṟa kaḷippuṇarcciyōṭu ceṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ. poykikaḷ tōṟum nīlam, kaḻunīr, neytal mutaliya pūkkaḷ malarnta nilimāṟi, ‘utayaṇaṉ iṉpamāka nakar ceṉṟaṭika’ eṉṟu kikūppi vēṇṭik koḷḷuvatupōlak kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir kūppiya karameṉak kuvintaṉa. vāṉa veḷi eṅkum paṟantu tirinta paṟavik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ palavaki olikaḷuṭaṉ tattam kūṭukaḷi aṭintaṉa. pakaliṉ vemmi taṇintu iraviṉ meṉkuḷir eṅkum paraviyatu. mālip poḻutu, ulaki iṉpamayakkam ceytuviṭṭu viṭi peṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. iravuk kaṉṉi nīlak karumpaṭṭāṭi pōrttu vantāḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya payaṇam toṭarntatu. pakivarkaḷip paṟṟiya kavaliyum mella mella maṟintatu. cūḻniliyum nēramum oṉṟu kūṭum pōtu paṟṟi niṟkum tollikaḷi maṟantu cuṟṟiyuḷḷa aḻaki iracikka ārampittal maṉita iyalpu. utayaṇaṉum vācavatattiyum kāñcaṉiyum vayantakaṉum maṭṭum itaṟku viti vilakkā eṉṉa? paki piṉpaṟṟit turattiyatu utayaṇaṉi. avaṉō taṉ payaṇattic cūḻnili aḻakil muḻukka muḻukka īṭupaṭuttik koṇṭē naṭattukiṟāṉ.

“nāḷi eppaṭiyum tattiyik kōcāmpi nakaril utayaṇaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉi āyamakaḷir etirkoṇṭaḻikkupaṭik kōcampi ceṉṟaṭivēṉ” eṉṟa uṟutiyi mēṟkoṇṭatupōla virintu ceṉṟatu. pattirāpati eṉṉum anta yāṉi. ivvāṟu virintu ōṭiya piṭiyiṉ oṭṭam, meṉmiyi aṉṟi vēṟu uṇarvukaḷip payiṉṟu aṟiyāta tattikkut tuṉpam koṭuttatu. piṭi ovvōr aṭivikkum pōtum pūviṉum melliya avaḷ uṭal kuluṅki āṭiyatu. anta āṭṭam uṭalvaliyi uṇṭākkiṟṟu. atu poṟāmal neṭṭuyirntu aṭikkaṭi vimmiṉāḷ vācavatatti. piṭiyiṉ puyalvēkam melliyal makaḷākiya tattikkuc cōkam viḷittatu. aṭikkaṭi neṭṭuyirtta tattiyik kāñcaṉamāli taḻuvik koṇṭāḷ. aṭikkaṭi piṭi kuluṅkiya vēkattil tatti, utayaṇaṉit tīṇṭa nērntatu. āṭavar uṭalit tīṇṭi yaṟiyāta kaṉṉi payirppu eytiṉāḷ. uṭal valiyil viḷinta cōkam oru puṟam. utayaṇaṉit tīṇṭum iṉpaṅ kalanta cōkam oru puṟam.

tattiyiṉ ciṉakkuṟippi, avaḷ kūṟāmalē terintu koṇṭa kāñcaṉamāli, avaḷit tāṅkiya vaṇṇamē cila ceytikaḷi mella avaḷukkuk kūṟiṉāḷ: “utayaṇaṉ uṉṉi iṉṉum muṟippaṭi nāṭaṟiya naṉmaṇam ceytu koḷḷavilli. āṉālum avaṉ uṉakku yāḻ kaṟpitta āciriyaṉ. āciriyaṉāka iruntatuṭaṉ uṉ maṉattik kavarnta kātaṟ kaḷvaṉuṅkūṭa. uṉakkup payirppu ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭiyatu avaciyamilli. uṉ tanti taṉ kippaṭa niṉṉi utayaṇaṉukku maṇam ceytu koṭukkavilliyē eṉpatu tāṉ kuṟi. āyiṉum uṉṉi avaṉ tāṉākavē aṭintu viṭṭāṉ, otta takutiyuḷḷa iruvar āciriyaṉum māṇākkiyumāka iruppiṉ maṇattilēyē antak kali niṟivuṟum eṉṟa periyōr vākku, uṅkaḷ variyil māṟṟaṟṟa uṇmiyāka miḷirkiṟatu. uṉakku mayakkatti uṇṭu paṇṇum piṭiyiṉ vēkattil cōkappaṭāmal tappa oru vaḻi kūṟuvēṉ” eṉṟivvāṟu kāñcaṉi tattikku urittup piṭicellum vēkattil mēlum kīḻum muṉṉum piṉṉumākac cuṟṟiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ cuḻaluvatik kāṇāmal kaṇṇi mūṭik koḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. appaṭik kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koṇṭāl mayakkam ēṟpaṭātu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. pattirāpati ōṭiya vēkattil cuṟṟiyirunta maraṅkaḷ piṉṉōkkic celvaṉa pōlavum etirē eḻuntu varuvaṉa pōlavum ticikaḷ cuḻaṟci eytiṉa. avaṟṟik kaṇṭāl talicuṟṟi mayakkam koḷḷa nērumākiyāl kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koṇṭāl mayakkam teriyāteṉṟu avaḷ tōḻi kūṟiyiruntāḷ. pattirāpatiyiṉ vēkattil uṭalāṭṭaṅ kaṇṭa tattikku neṟṟiyil muttu muttāka arumpiyirunta vērvit tuḷikaḷit tuṭittāḷ kāñcaṉi. uṭal cōrntu kāṇappaṭṭa tattiyi mella utayaṇaṉiṉ paranta mārpiṟ cārttiṉāḷ kāñcaṉi. karuṭap paṟaviyiṉ ciṟakukaḷ olippatippōlap piṭi ovvoru aṭiyit tariyil vikkumpōtum mēlē uṭkārntiruntavarkaḷukkut tiṭum tiṭum eṉṟa oli kēṭṭu atircci ceytatu. anta oli vācavatattiyiṉ kātukaḷil viḻātapaṭi avaḷ kātukaḷil pañcu aṭittuk kāval koṇṭāḷ kāñcaṉa māli. evvaḷavu pēṇiyum tattikku anta vēkam cōkattiyē mēṉmēlum koṭuttuk koṇṭiruntatu. atu kaṇṭa kāñcaṉi tattiyiṭam utayaṇaṉip paṟṟik koṇṭirukkumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tirumpi utayaṇaṉip pārttu, vēkattic ciṟitu taṇikkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. avaṉum atu kēṭṭu vēkattic caṟṟukkuṟittāṉ. vācavatattiyiṉ tuvaṇṭa pūvuṭal paranta mārpukku iṭṭamāli pōl avaṉmēl mella cāyntatu.





16. vēkattil viḷinta cōkam

aḻakiya peruvayalkaḷilē koṭṭum paṟiyolikkup payanta paciyakaṇkaḷi yuṭiya erumi taṉ kaṉṟi niṉintu maṭi niṟinta iṉiya pāli, tāmarip poykiyiṉ virinta ilikaḷil, eḻiliḷa aṉṉaṅkaḷōṭu nārik kuñcukaḷum uṇṇa eḻuntu varumāṟu corintaṉa. periya periya pāttikaḷil ōṅki vaḷarnta karumpiṉ maṭalkaḷilē kaṭṭappaṭṭa ceṟinta iṉiya tēṉ kūṭṭil iruntu oḻukum centēṉ, kīḻē pakkattil kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci tarum tāmaric cemmalaril tīyi vaḷarppataṟkuc ceyyum ākuti pōlap poḻintatu. iṭamakaṉṟa cōlikaḷilē veḷḷik kumpameṉak kulitaḷḷiya pāḷik kamukum, pacceṉap parutta mūṅkilum, paḻutta katali maraṅkaḷum, kottut kottākak kuli taḷḷiya teṉṉi marak kūṭṭamum, iṉiya tīm palāvum, tē māmaraṅkaḷum, puṉṉiyum, cevantiyum, poṉṉeṉa iṇartta pūṅkuvi ñāḻalum, iṉṉum palapala maraṅkoṭi ceṭikaḷiṉ īṭṭamum kalanta tōṟṟam eḻumiyum nukara ariya iṉpak kāṭciyāka viḷaṅkiṉa. pakaṟpōtilē katiravaṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷum uḷnuḻiya iyalāta ceṟivu porunti iruṇṭavi, accōlikaḷ. kaḻaṉikaḷ tōṟum muṟṟic cāyntirunta viḷi neṟkatirkaḷ kāṟṟōṭu acintāṭiṉa. aṅkaṅkē uḻavar kūṭṭam ceyyum āravāramum, kaḷaṅkaḷil nellaṭippōr ceyyum kaḷic ceyalkaḷum kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. vēḷāḷa makaḷir vaḷarkkum illuṟi kiḷikaḷiṉ maḻalic colviruntu orupuṟam; taṇṇumiyum taṭāriyumāka muḻavuṭaṉ muḻakkum āṭavar nātaveḷḷam orupuṟam, kaḷi kaṭṭum kaṭiciyarkaḷ varip pāṭal pāṭum ici viruntu iṉṉoru puṟam. ittakiya maruta nilap peruvaḻiyil pattirāpati utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōric cumantu virivākac ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu.

avarkaḷ appōtu aruṭṭa nakaratti aṇukik koṇṭiruntaṉar. etirē periya kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷum, vāṉaḷāviya koṭi matilkaḷumākat terinta aruṭṭa nakarattiṉ puṟakkāṭci oru taṉi aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu. tāṉ celvatu akkam pakkattiluḷḷōr aṟitaṟku anta iravil ētuvākiviṭak kūṭātu eṉpataṟkāka aruṭṭa nakaratti aṇukumpōtu, pattirāpati aṇintirunta maṇiyi olikkātapaṭi ceytu nīkkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattiyum kāñcaṉiyum aṇintirunta kiṇkiṇic cilampukaḷiṉ kīta oliyik kūṭat taṭukkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. iruḷ nēramākiyāl ēṉiyōr aiyuṟa nērum. ataṉāl yātu viḷiyiṉum viḷiyum eṉṟu karutiyē utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu ceytāṉ. aruṭṭa nakaratti neruṅkiyatum nakariṉ valap pakkamāka oru peruvaḻi caṟṟu otuṅkic ceṉṟatu. iṭap puṟam nakari oṭṭi atēpōl oru vaḻi amintiruntatu. uṭaṉ irunta vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉ nōkki, “iṭap pakkamākac ceṉṟāl aruṭṭa nakarattār nammuṭiya celavi aṟiya nēriṭum. ataṉāl namakkut tuṉpam varalām. ākiyāl olikaḷiṉṟi amitiyāka valap puṟam cellum peruvaḻiyilē piṭiyic celuttuka” eṉṟāṉ. aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka miṉukkik koṇṭirunta nakariṉ māṭap peruviḷakkukaḷ iruḷiṭiyē terintaṉa. kaṇīr kaṇīreṉṟutēva kōṭṭaṅkaḷil iruntu maṇiyoli eḻuntu paraviya vaṇṇa miruntatu. utayaṇaṉ vayantakaṉ kūṟiyavāṟē pattirāpatiyi mella valap pakkattu vaḻiyiṟ celuttiṉāṉ. iṭi yiṭiyē vayantakaṉ aṅkuḷḷa vaḻikaḷip paṟṟiya virivāṉa ceytikaḷi utayaṇaṉukku viḷakkik kūṟiṉāṉ. vaḻikaḷ iraṇṭākap pirivatu maṉa orumiyillātavar kaḷuṭiya niliyi ottiruntatu. vayantakaṉ tuṇi, vaḻi kāṭṭum peruntuṇiyāka oḷikāṭṭi amintatu utayaṇaṉukku. neṭunēram payaṇam ceytu kaḻintapiṉ mulli nilamoṉṟu etirppaṭṭatu.

mullik koṭikaḷ, mullinila makaḷir iṭi pōlak koṭi parappi, naki pōla malarntu viḷaṅkiṉa. kuravu taḷavu kuruntam mutaliyaṉa mella malarntu tōṉṟiṉa. avikaḷ malarum cevvi nōkkik kāttirunta vaṇṭukaḷ, makiḻntu tēṉ viruntuṇṇap puṟappaṭṭaṉa. tiṉiyum cōḷamum vittiṭṭu viḷikkum mulli nilamakkaḷ avaṟṟi variciyākap payiriṭṭirunta kāṭci kaviṉ ceṟintu tōṉṟiyatu. an nilattil viḷiyattakka vēṟu cila pala tāṉiyaṅkaḷiyum payiriṭṭiruntaṉar. mulli nila makkaḷ muyaṟcimikka paṇpiṉar eṉpati atu viḷakkiyatu. kollip puṟaṅkaḷilē mēyntu koṇṭirunta mulli nilattup pacukkaḷ maṭiniṟinta pālic cēṟṟu nilattil poḻintaṉa. iraliyum piṇiyumākiya māṉkaḷ mucuṇṭik koṭiyiṉ paciya taḻiyi mēyntavaṇṇam tirintaṉa. kāyāmpūvum koṉṟiyum kaviṉip pūttu viḷaṅkum poḻilkaḷil iṭi iṭiyē ciṟuciṟu tōṭṭa vīṭukaḷ viḷaṅkiṉa. kuṟṟamaṟṟa vāḻkki kōvalar vāḻkki. pacukkaḷik kāttu ōmpip payaṉpeṟum vāḻkki allavā atu? attakiya nalvāḻvu paṭitta mulli nilattik kaṭantu kuṟiñci nilap pakutiyi aṭintatu piṭi.

maliccāravi neruṅkiviṭṭataṟku aṟikuṟiyākac ‘cō’veṉṟu vīḻum aruvikaḷiṉ iṉṉici cevikaḷukku viruntu ceytatu. aṅkumiṅkumākat tāvi ōṭum kavarimāṉkaḷiṉ kāṭciyil mikka aḻakik kaṇṭārkaḷ. ceṇpakamum acōkamum ceṟintu vaḷarntiruntaṉa. kuṟiñciyum vēṅkiyum kuḷirap pūttiruntaṉa. eṅkum pūmaṇam, kāṟṟil kalantu vīciṟṟu. akil cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ naṉṟāka muṟṟi veṭittiruntataṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ maṇamvēṟu tammi nukarac ceytaṉa. eṅku nōkkiṉum tīñcuṉikaḷ. maliyil ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil vēṭar iṭṭirunta tī antak kaṅkul yāmattil tōṉṟiya putiyatoru katiravaṉ eṉa viḷaṅkiyatu. ivvaḷavu vaḷam poruntiya kuṟiñci nilatti nūṟṟirupattintu kāta elliyum kaṭantu mēṟceṉṟatu pattirāpati. etirē akaṉṟa peruṅkarikaḷ iraṇṭiṟkum naṭuvē kalakalaveṉṟa oliyuṭaṉ narumatiyāṟu oṭikkoṇṭiruntatu. naṭuvil iṭiveḷi yiṉṟi irumaruṅkum nīr nirampik koṇṭirunta narumati nati, palavaki vaḷaṅkaḷum poruntiyatu. pala vaḷaṅkaḷiyum antap piratēcattiṟku aḷikkak kūṭiyatumkūṭa. ataṉ iru karikaḷilum aṭarnta marak kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aṇivakuttu niṟpaṉapōla amintiruntaṉa. antak karikaḷil eppōtum āravārattukkup pañcamirukkātu. nīrt tuṟip paratavar makiḻcci āravārattuṭaṉ paṟavikaḷiṉ kuralkaḷ pōṭṭi pōṭum iṭam atu. naṟumaṇam poruntiya malarc cōlikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭantu narumatik kariyi aṭintatum piṉpuṟamāka amarntirunta vayantakaṉ piṭiyiliruntu kīḻē iṟaṅkiṉāṉ. natiyip pārtta vayantakaṉ taṇṇīr appōtu mikutiyāka oṭukiṟatu eṉpati aṟintukoṇṭāṉ. poym maṇalāl uḷḷē āḻttiviṭātatum caṟṟu āḻam kuṟivāṉatumāṉa oru pakutiyil iṟaṅki vayantakaṉ muṉṉē vaḻikāṭṭi nīntic ceṉṟāṉ. piṉṉē piṭiceṉṟatu. tukka mayakkattil irunta vācavatattiyik kīḻē naḻuvaviṭātapaṭi iṟukat taḻuvik koṇṭu piṭiyik kavaṉamāka āṟṟil celuttiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. kāñcaṉi piṉpuṟamākat tattiyit tāṅkik koṇṭāḷ. piṭi mellak kariyēṟiyatu.

narumatiyiṉ ak kariyi aṭintatum vayantakaṉ nīril naṉintirunta taṉatu uṭalit tuvaṭṭikkoṇṭu piṭimītu ēṟikkoṇṭāṉ. vāṉattil viḷaṅkiya naṭcattiraṅkaḷik kūrntu nōkkiyapiṉ appōtu iravu evvaḷavu nēramāki irukkum eṉpatik kaṇakkiṭṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. piṭiyiṉ niliyum atu kaṭanta āṟṟiṉ aḷavum ivi yāvum cērntu, vayantakaṉukku ōruṇmiyip pulappaṭuttiṉa. eppaṭiyum pattirāpati iṉṉum ōriraṇṭu kātam celvataṟkuḷ taṅkaḷik kiviṭṭu viṭum eṉpatutāṉ anta uṇmi. narumatiyāṟṟiṟku appāl oru periya pālivaṉam, caṟṟut tolivil iruntatu. antap pālinilam payaṅkarattiṟkup pēr peṟṟatu. aṅkaṅkē tōṉṟiya parukkik kaṟkaḷiṉ karaṭumuraṭāṉa tōṟṟamum orē maṇaṟ peruveḷiyum antap pālivaṉattiṟku vantu viṭṭōm eṉpati vayantakaṉukku aṟivuṟuttiṉa. avaṉ maṉattiṟ cila accaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ‘cumantu vanta yāṉi virivil cōrntuviṭum’ eṉpatit tavira, antap pāli nila vaḻiyiṉ payaṅkaramum vayantakaṉukku niṉivu vantatu.

koṭumi, koli, koḷḷi ivikaḷukku antap pāli nilavaḻi iruppiṭam. aṅkē vacikkum āṟali kaḷvar īvirakkamaṟṟa koliñar. vaḻi ōramāka amintirunta pāḻaṭinta turkki kōyilil patuṅkiyiruntu vaḻiyil varuvōr pōvōrik koḷḷiyaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttuvatu avarkaḷ vaḻakkam. kollappaṭṭa uṭalkaḷip parukkik kaṟkaḷāl mūṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. veṭṭuṇṭa uṭalkaḷik kuḻi tōṇṭip putittuviṭuvar. anta varaṇṭupōṉa pāli nilattip pōṉṟatutāṉ avarkaḷ uḷḷamum. kaḷḷi, muḷḷi mutaliya pālinilattu maraṅkaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē vaḷarntiruntaṉa. kuḷirp paruvattilum veppak koṭumi tāṅka muṭiyāta iṭam atu. ittakiya tuṉpaṅkaḷ niṟintatu. ākiyāl poḻutu pularvataṟkuḷ nalla iruḷilēyē atik kaṭantuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. atik kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ pattirāpatiyi muṉṉilum virivāka muṭukkiṉāṉ. piṭi kāreṉak kaṭitu ceṉṟatu. ataṉ acura vēkattil vayantakaṉ kiyilirunta kōṭapati eṉṟa yāḻ kīḻē vīḻntu mūṅkiṟ putar oṉṟilē cikkik koṇṭatu. yāḻ viḻuntati vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉiṭam kūṟik koṇṭirukkumpōtē, piṭi nūṟu viṟkiṭi tūram muṉṉāl kaṭantu ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. pirama cuntaramuṉivarāl taṉakku aḷikkappaṭṭa aṟputamāṉa teyvīka yāḻākiya kōṭapatiyi utayaṇaṉ iḻantuviṭṭāṉ.

yāḻit tavaṟaviṭṭa iṭattik kaṭantu mikka tolivu vantu viṭṭati aṟinta utayaṇaṉ, “tanta teyvamtāṉē tarum! ataṟkāka mīṇṭum vaḻiyil tirumpa muṭiyātu” eṉṟurittuviṭṭut tayaṅkāmal mēlē pattirāpatiyic celuttiṉāṉ. kōṭapatitāṉ utayaṇaṉukkut teyva yāṉiyip paṇipuriyac ceytatu. naḷakiriyiṉ matatti aṭakkip piraccōtaṉaṉiṭam naṉmatippup peṟavum, tattikku yāḻ kaṟpittuk kātal koḷḷavum ceytatu. anta arum poruḷ keṭṭuppōy viṭṭatu. āṉālum utayaṇaṉ maṉaṅ kalaṅkiṉāṉilli. ciṟu tuyarukku varuntip perun tuyarattiṟku naṭuvē akappaṭṭuk koḷḷak kūṭātallavā? eṉavē maṉantēṟi mēlē ceṉṟaṉar. vēkattil viḷinta cōkaṅkaḷi vēkattālēyē maṟakka muyaṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





17. piṭiyiṉ vīḻcci

kōṭapatiyi iḻanta tuyaratti maṟakka avarkaḷ muyaṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu vēṟōr putiya tuyaram pukuntu vāṭṭiyatu. eṇpatelli aḷavu ōṭiyapiṉ piṭi melivaṭintu taḷarntatu. piṭiyiṉ naṭiyil vēkam kuṟintatu. kālkaḷum tutikkiyum vitir vitirppat taḷḷāṭiyatu pattirāpati. ataṉ niliyi uyttuṇarnta utayaṇaṉ pāli nilattil eñciyuḷḷa irupatu ellit tolivum eppaṭiyāvatu atik koṇṭē kaṭantuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇit turitappaṭuttiṉāṉ. ippōtō, iṉṉum caṟṟu nērattilō piṭi iṟantu pōvatu uṟuti eṉpatiyum aṅki nelliyeṉa avaṉ aṟintāṉ.

yāṉikaḷiṉ iyalpip pala nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilākat tērntirunta utayaṇaṉ, inta uṇmiyi aṟintiruntatu viyappiṟkuriya ceyti alla. āṉāl, cettukkoṇṭirukkum antap piṭiyiṉ tuṇikoṇṭē pāli nilattiyum kaṭantu piracōtaṉaṉuṭiya nāṭṭu elliyiyum nīṅkiyatutāṉ viyappiṟku uriyatu. piṭiyip paṟṟiyirunta nōy ‘kālakūṭam’ eṉṉum kaṭunōyākiyāl iṉimēl atu piḻikka ev vakiyilum vaḻiyē illi. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭiya nāṭākiya ainnūṟṟelli nila aḷaviyum kaṭantu nōyiṉ kaṭumi mikunta piṭi, ‘yāṉ iṉi uyir viṭuvēṉ. niṉṉuyirkku ētum tuṉpam nērāmal kāttuk koṇarntu viṭṭēṉ. cukamāka nī iṉi mēlē cellalām’ eṉṟu kūṟuvatupōlat taṉ uṭal veṭaveṭakkac cōrvu eyti niṉṟatu. ēṟkeṉavē piṭiyiṉ niliyi narumatiyāṟṟaṅ kariyil tīrmāṉittirunta vayantakaṉ eṉ ceyvatu eṉṉum kuṟipput tōṉṟa utayaṇaṉ mukattip pārttāṉ. “piṭi nammi viriviṟ kiviṭṭuviṭum pōlirukkiṟatu” eṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. “teppattilē nīriṭic celvōri atu kaviḻntu iṭiyilēyē kiviṭuvatupōla āyiṟṟu nam katiyum” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉi nōkki mēlum kūṟi viṭṭuk kāñcaṉiyi viriviṟ piṭiyiṉ piṉpuṟamāka iṟaṅkumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāṉ. vayantakaṉ muṉpē iṟaṅki viṭṭāṉ. taṉ villiyum ampaṟāttūṇiyiyum iṟukkik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟoru kiyāl cōrnta niliyilirunta vācavatattiyi mārpuṟat taḻuvik koṇṭē kīḻē iṟaṅkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ak kāṭci, kariya mali yoṉṟiliruntu nilattilē arttanārīcuvaraṉākac civaperumāṉ iṟaṅki varuvatupōliruntatu. appōtu umātēviyāri oru puṟattē koṇṭu tōṉṟum mukkaṭ kaṭavuḷ pōlk kāṭci koṭuttāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

ivarkaḷ ivvāṟu iṟaṅkiyatum, piṭi mellak kīḻē vīḻntatu. pakkattu vilāppuṟam tariyilē paṭumpaṭi cāyntu vīḻiṉ, aṅkē niṉṟu koṇṭirukkum utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōrukkut tuṉpam varumeṉṟeṇṇik kālkaḷit tariyil pāvi amarvatupōla vīḻntatu atu. utayaṇaṉukkup pakkattil cāyntu vīḻntatu, avaṉi vaṇaṅkuvatu pōlavum viṭi peṟṟuk koḷvatupōlavum iruntatu. nīṇṭa kariya perum paṭṭuppi oṉṟiliruntu pavaḻattik koṭṭuvatupōla ataṉ tutikkiyil iruntu irattam vaṭintu koṇṭiruntatu. piṭi vīḻnta vakiyum ticiyum koṇṭu, taṉakku mutalil taṉ nāṭu cellum vaḻiyil, tuṉpam vantu piṉ nīṅkumeṉṟu utayaṇaṉ aṟintāṉ. ataṉ cāvu uṟuti eṉṟu terintu koṇṭatum, atu aṇintirunta maṇi, purōcik kayiṟu, mutukiliṭṭirunta metti, mēṟ pōrtta paṭṭukkaliṅkam ivikaḷi utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉ iruvarumākak kaḷintaṉar. appaṭik kaḷiyumpōtu, mikunta aṉpuṭaṉ ataṉ mattakattik kikuḷirat taṭavik koṭuttāṉ utayaṇaṉ. appōtu kīḻ vāṉattil viṭiveḷḷi mēlē eḻuntu tōṉṟik koṇṭiruntatu. viṭivataṟku iṉṉum iraṇṭu nāḻikip poḻutu irukkum. tattiyum kāñcaṉamāliyum tuyiliṉṟi mikac cōrvākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. “avarkaḷ iruvariyum iṅkē pakkattil eṅkāvatu oriṭattil tuyilac ceytu nī kāval purika” eṉṟu vayantakaṉukku āṇi yiṭṭuviṭṭu iṟanta pattirāpati naṟkati aṭitaṟporuṭṭuc ceyyavēṇṭiya kaṭaṉkaḷic ceyyalāṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. pakkattilē irunta nīr niṟinta aḻakiya tāmarip poyki yoṉṟil pōy nīrāṭiṉāṉ. nīrāṭiyapiṉ tūya uḷḷattōṭu taṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvattik karuti nīrkkaṭaṉiyum niyamakkiriyikaḷiyum ceytu muṭittāṉ. piṉṉar vayantakaṉ irukkumiṭam ceṉṟu tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷōṭu pakaṟpoḻutil maṟintu vacippataṟku uriya or iṭattik kāṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉuṭaṉ kalantu ālōcittāṉ. utayaṇaṉuṭiya iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷil vayantakaṉukku muṟṟilum paṅku uṇṭu. avarkaḷiṭiyē irunta naṭpu avvaḷaviṟku uyarntatu.

utayaṇaṉ kūṟiyatik kēṭṭa vayantakaṉ, anta iṭattiṉuṭiya niliyiyum aṅkiruntu tāṅkaḷ uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭuc cellavēṇṭiya avaciyattiyum vivaramākak kūṟiṉāṉ. “maṉanekiḻcci eṉpatu caṟṟaḷavumillāta koṭu viṉi vāḻkkiyi uṭiya vēṭarkaḷ payilum iṭam itu. aṉṟiyum iraṇṭu nāṭukaḷ cantikkum ellit tāṉamākiya iṅkiruppatu tuṉpatti valitiṉ aḻippatu pōlākum. vācavatatti varuntiṉālum tuṉpattip poruṭpaṭuttātu mēlē naṭattalē cari” eṉṟu vayantakaṉ colli muṭittāṉ. utayaṇaṉ kāñcaṉiyiṭam atik kūṟi naṭantu puṟappaṭumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. vācavatattiyiṉ niliyō eḻuntiruntu mēlē kāli or aṭikūṭa eṭuttu vikka ēṟṟatāka illi. naṭip paḻakkamē aṟiyāta pūṅkoṭi tatti. kātalaṉ maṉak karuttaṟintu naṭakka uṭaṉpaṭṭu eḻuntāḷ.

taṉatu pañciṉum melliya malaraṭikaḷ civappat tatti kāñcaṉiyik kippaṟṟik koṇṭu tuvaḷat tuvaḷa mella naṭantāḷ. vayantakaṉ vaḻikāṭṭa utayaṇaṉ muṉ ceṉṟāṉ. naṭakka naṭakkat tattiyiṉ melivu atikappaṭuvatu kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉi uṭaṉaṭiyāka aṅkē taṅkuvataṟku oriṭam pārkkap paṇittāṉ. vayantakaṉ cuṟṟum muṟṟum pārtta vaṇṇam ōriṭam tēṭiṉāṉ. vācavatatti cōrntu uṭkārntuviṭṭāḷ. poḻutuviṭintu veyilum ēṟat toṭaṅkiyiruntatu.

pakkattil pāṟi pāṟiyākak kiṭanta kal pakutiyiṉiṭiyē oru nīrppoyki iruntatu. ataṉ kariyi oṭṭik kōṅkilava maraṅkaḷ cila aṭarttiyāka vaḷarn tiruntaṉa. putarpōlac ceṟintu viḷaṅkiya anta ilavamarak kūṭṭam, vayantakaṉ nōkkil viḻuntatu. ‘maṟivākat taṅkiyiruppataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam atutāṉ’ eṉṟu tīrmāṉam ceytu koṇṭavaṉāy avaṉ ati neruṅkiṉāṉ. maraṅkaḷiṉ mēlē cenniṟap paṭṭāl kūṭāram amittatu pōlac civanta ilavampū kottuk kottākap pūttiruntatu. putarukku uṭpuṟam oru kukipōla, iruppataṟku vacatiyāka amintuḷḷamiyik kaṇṭa vayantakaṉ, aṅkē utirntirunta carukukaḷiyum muḷ poruntiya kiḷikaḷiyum orupuṟamāka otukkiviṭṭup paciya ilikaḷik koṇṭu tittu ākkappaṭṭa virippu oṉṟit tariyil virittāṉ. ataṟkuḷ utayaṇaṉum tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷum aṅkē vantaṉar. yāvarum anta nāḷiṉ naṇpakaṟ poḻuti maṟivāṉa avviṭattil kaḻikka virumpiṉar. pakkattilirunta poykiyiṉ kuḷircci aṅkē avarkaḷukkuc caṟṟē cōrvu nīkki iṉpam aḷittatu.





﻿18. neruṅkiya tuṉpam

pakaṟpoḻutu oru vaḻiyākap poykik kariyil anta ilavam putariṭiyē kaḻintuviṭṭatu. aḻaṟkuḻampeṉa mēlvāṉam civappuṟa antip poḻutu mella vantatu. paṟavikaḷellām palvēṟu olikaḷic ceytu koṇṭē tattam kūṭukaḷi aṭintaṉa. pakkattup poykiyil katiravaṉukkuk kikūppi viṭi koṭuppaṉa pōla malarkaḷ kuvintaṉa. vayantakaṉ, utayaṇaṉi nōkki mēlē eṉṉa ceyyalām eṉpatu paṟṟic cila vivaraṅkaḷik kūṟiṉāṉ. “iruṭpōtu vantuviṭṭatāl, antak kāṭṭi aṭuttirukkum urumaṇṇuvāviṉāl āḷappaṭum cayanti nakarattukkuk kūṭap pōvataṟkilli. iruḷip poruṭpaṭuttātu ceṉṟāl pala tuṉpaṅkaḷ nērum. tattiyō mikavum valuviḻantu kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ. ākiyāl ippōtu cellutal naṉṟaṉṟu. yāṉ nām naṇpaṉākiya iṭavakaṉāl āḷappaṭum puṭpaka nakaram ceṉṟu, namatu utavikku oru paṭiyum piṟavacatikaḷum peṟṟuk kāliyil varuvēṉ. atuvari nī ivarkaḷōṭu iṅkēyē iru” eṉṟu vayantakaṉ kūṟiviṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ.

puṭpaka nakaram puṟappaṭat taṉṉiṭam viṭi peṟṟuk koṇṭa vayantakaṉi nōkki utayaṇaṉ cila kūṟiṉāṉ: “iṭavakaṉum yāṉumē aṟinta aṭiyāḷac ceyti oṉṟu uṇṭu. atik kūṟiṉāloḻiya niṉṉi avaṉ nampa māṭṭāṉ. nī nam niliyiyum nikaḻnta yāvaṟṟiyum iṭavakaṉiṭam kūṟip paṭiyutaviyum piṟavum peṟṟu varal vēṇṭum. in nērattil nām uṟutiyāka nāṭu tirumpuvatu iṭavakaṉiṭam nī peṟṟu varum utaviyip poṟuttē irukkiṟatu” eṉṟu ivvāṟu urittu, vayantakaṉiṭam anta aṭiyāḷac ceytiyiyum kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ utayaṇaṉi vaṇaṅkiviṭṭup puṭpaka nakaram puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟāṉ. vayantakaṉ puṟappaṭumpōtu iravu nēram ārampamāki viṭṭatu. vayantakaṉi aṉuppiyapiṉ utayaṇaṉ tattiyiyum kāñcaṉiyiyum ilavam putariṉ uṭpakutiyil nimmatiyākat turaṅkumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tāṉ veḷippuṟam kāvalāka niṉṟu koṇṭāṉ. uḷḷē tuyilum tattiyiṉ eḻiṟ kāṭciyil taṉṉuṭiya tuṉpaṅkaḷi yellām maṟantavaṉāki iruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. nīṇṭa anta iravu muḻutum avaṉ turaṅkavē illi. valakkarattil ēntiya vāḷuṭaṉē poḻutu pularumvari kākka vēṇṭiya kaṭami avaṉukku iruntatu. poḻutum oruvāṟu pularntatu. ilava marattuk kiḷiyoṉṟilirunta mayil, paccōnti yoṉṟu taṉmēl vālic cuḻaṟṟikkoṇṭu varuvatukaṇṭu kataṟuvatupōlak kattik koṇṭiruntatu. kiḻakkē aṭivāṉam civantatu. utayaṇaṉ kaṇkaḷ tūkkattāl cuḻaṉṟaṉa. tūkkatti viṭuttup poykiyil nīrāṭik kālik kaṭaṉkaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ avaṉ.

kālik kaṭaṉkaḷi muṭittuviṭṭut taṉ taṉimi niliyiyum taṉakku variciyāka nērum tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum eṇṇiyavāṟē puṭpakanakar pōṉa vayantakaṉi etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. tattiyiṉ cōrnta nili kaṇṭu avaḷit tēṟṟip pēṇumāṟu kāñcaṉikkuk kūṟiviṭṭu, vaḻiyōramāka nakarntu caṟṟut toliviliruntu eṅkāvatu vayantakaṉatu paṭi varukiṟatā, eṉṟu pārkkap puṟappaṭṭa utayaṇaṉi oru tīya nimittam taṭi ceytatu. ilava marattiṉ ucciyilē amarntirunta vayavaṉ eṉṉum paṟaviyoṉṟu palamuṟi kattiyatu. atu kural koṭuttatiliruntu virivil evarō pakivar paṭi taṉṉi neruṅka irukkiṟatu eṉṟaṟintāṉ utayaṇaṉ. mutaṉ mutalil tāṉ piraccōtaṉaṉāl vañcaka yāṉiyiṉ mūlam ciṟip piṭikkap paṭuvataṟku muṉpuṅkūṭa itē paṟavi kattiya nimittam avaṉukku niṉivil eḻuntatu. etaṟkum muṉṉēṟpāṭāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa karuttiṉaṉāy villit tirutti atiṟ poruntiya ampuṭaṉ ilavam putariṉ muṉpu niṉṟu koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ. iḵtu ivvāṟu irukkap pattirāpati muṉpu iṟantu viḻuntu kiṭanta vaḻiyōramāka vanta koḷḷiyaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttum toḻiliyuṭiya vēṭar cilar cantēkamuṟṟu niṉṟaṉar. antap pakutiyil maṟintu vāḻpavarkaḷākiya avarkaḷ vaḻiyiṟ cellum vaṇikar kūṭṭaṅkaḷik koḷḷiyaṭippatum koli ceyvatumē toḻilāka uṭiyavarkaḷ. cavarar, puḷiñar eṉṟa anta iruvaki iṉattu vēṭarkaḷum aṉṟu kāliyil pattirāpatiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭu kaṇṭu, atip piṉpaṟṟiyē patuṅkip patuṅki vantaṉar. aṭiccuvaṭu kaṇṭatum atu ētō oru nāṭṭup piṭiyiṉuṭiya aṭiccuvaṭu eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut terintu viṭṭatu. atil ceṉṟōr celvamikkavarāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum aṉumāṉittaṉar. cuvaṭu paṟṟi vanta avarkaḷ pattirāpati vīḻntu kiṭanta iṭattiṟkē vantu cērntaṉar. aṅkē āṭavar peṇṭir kāl aṭikaḷiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭa avarkaḷ cantēkam uṟutip paṭṭatu. aṅṅaṉam cantēkan tōṉṟiṉālum yāṉiyil vantavarkaḷ itaṟkuḷ veku tolivu ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum. ākiyāl avarkaḷiṭam koḷḷi aṭippataṟku iyalāmaṟ pōyiṟṟē eṉṟu vēṭar varunti urittaṉar. avarkaḷ kūṟṟi maṟantu mutiya vēṭaṉākiya nimittikaṉ oruvaṉ, paṟavi oṉṟiṉ oli nimittattik kēṭṭu “nīṅkaḷ niṉippatu tavaṟu. ip piṭiyil vantōr iṅkētāṉ pakkattē oriṭattilē taṅkiyirukka vēṇṭuma. nām virintu ceṉṟāl avarkaḷik kāṇalām. perum poruḷ koḷḷiyākak kiṭṭum. āṉāl vīram mikka āṇ makaṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ kaṭiṉamākap pōriṭṭē ap poruḷi nām aṭimuṭiyum” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. avvāṟu kūṟi muṭikkavum neñcil īvirakkamaṟṟa anta vañcakar kūṭṭam oṉṟukūṭi eḻuntuviṭṭatu. piṭivīḻnta iṭattiliruntu cellum āṭavar peṇṭir aṭiccuvaṭukaḷi iṭi viṭāmal piṉpaṟṟiya vēṭar ilavam putari neruṅki viṭṭaṉar. aṅkē cuṟṟit cuṟṟit tēṭiṉar. appōtu poykikku etirē ilavam putariṉ vāyilil niṉṟu koṇṭirunta utayaṇaṉic cilar pārttu viṭṭaṉar. “atō atō, akappaṭṭuk koṇṭāṉ” eṉṟa kūkkuraluṭaṉ ilavam putaric cūḻntukoṇṭu tākka ārampittaṉar vēṭar. utayaṇaṉ taṉṉi etirttuc cūḻum pakivari vil muṉiyil ampu maḻi poḻintu viraṭṭa ārampikkummuṉ putarukkuḷ orumuṟi tirumpip pārttāṉ. oṉṟum puriyāta kalavarattuṭaṉē kaṇkaḷil accam oḷira naṭukkattōṭu veḷiyē nōkkik koṇṭirunta tattiyik kākkumpaṭi, uḷḷē uṭaṉirunta kāñcaṉikkuk kūṟiviṭṭu villi vaḷittāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

etirtta vēṭar, itu kaṇṭu tikittu niṉṟaṉar. tikippu orupuṟam iruntālum eḷitākak kiyil cikkiya koḷḷiyi viṭṭuviṭa avarkaḷ virumpavum illi. taṉiyoruvaṉāka niṉṟa utayaṇaṉi neruṅki etirttu veṟṟi koḷḷa muyaṉṟārkaḷ. “yāṉi iṟantu pōṉatum iṅkiruntu tappi nāṭu ceṉṟuviṭalām eṉṟu karutiṉāy pōlum! nī eppaṭip pōyviṭa muṭiyum? uṉ uyiri uṇṇāmal uṉṉi viṭṭuviṭa māṭṭōm. mariyātiyāka nī yār eṉpatic colliviṭu” eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē avaṉi vāṭṭa ārampittatu vēṭṭuvar kūṭṭam.

avarkaḷ ceyta ventuyarkaḷukkum kēṭṭa kēḷvikaḷukkum utayaṇaṉ vāy tiṟantu patil collavē illi; avaṉ kiyilirunta villē viṭi kūṟik koṇṭiruntatu. anta vīra villiliruntu noṭikku noṭi paṟantu koṇṭirunta ampukaḷ vēṭaril palari viṇṇukku aṉuppivittaṉa. camayamum cūḻniliyum etirpārāta tuṉpaṅkaḷit tarumpōtu, etirpārāta tuṇiviyum maṉitaṉukkuk koṭuttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kolik koṭumikaḷukkut taṉnikaraṟṟa kāṭṭu vēṭarkaḷi oṟṟi maṉitaṉāka niṉṟu etirkkum āṟṟalum utayaṇaṉukku appaṭittāṉ ēṟpaṭṭatu. cuṟṟi vaḷikkum palarkku iṭiyil utayaṇaṉ oruvaṉē palarāka niṉṟu, palarōṭum pōriṭṭa vintiyi niṉikka niṉikka etirikaḷukkē viyappūṭṭiyatu. taṅkaḷ cāmarttiyattit tavirap piṟar tiṟamiyi viyappati vāḻkkiyil iḻinta paṇpākak karutupavar ak kāṭṭu vēṭar. āṉāl, utayaṇaṉik kaṇṭa piṉṉar, antak koḷkiyit tāṅkaḷākavē avarkaḷ māṟṟik koḷḷa nērntatu. ivaṉ veṟum maṉitaṉtāṉā? allatu kūṟṟuvaṉiṉ vēṟṟuruvamō?’ eṉṟu aiyuṟavu koṇṭaṉar. villiyum ampiyum avaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷil putiya putiya nuṇukkaṅkaḷ avarkaḷukkup pulappaṭṭaṉa. oru noṭiyil cūḻntuḷḷa palarukkum atircci uṇṭākum vaṇṇam kaṇkaḷ cuṟṟic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu paṟakkum paṭiyāka avikaḷi utayaṇaṉ eyta muṟiyi viyavāta vēṭar illi. uḷḷē tatti mutaṉ mutalāka utayaṇaṉiṉ pōrttiṟattik kaṇṭu kātal urimi kalanta makiḻcciyōṭu viyantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. pōriṉ avaciyam taṉ poruṭṭeṉṟa tukkamum avaḷukku iruntatu.

vēṭarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa ēmāṟṟattik kōpamāka māṟṟikkoṇṭu, puḷnimittam kūṟiya mutiya vēṭaṉmēl atic celuttiṉārkaḷ. pakivaṉiṭattil arumperum tiṟaṉik kāṇkiṉṟa ovvoruvaṉum ati atika nēram viyakka muṭiyātu. caṟṟu nēram taṉṉi maṟanta niliyil tōṉṟum anta viyappuṇarcci vekuvirivil mikap periya acūyiyōṭukūṭiya poṟāmiyāka uruppeṟṟu viṭum. itu ulaka iyaṟki maṉita cupāvamuṅ kūṭa inta iyaṟkikku ak kāṭṭu vēṭarkaḷ viti vilakkā eṉṉa? illiyē! mika virivil taṅkaḷi uṇarntu camāḷittukkoṇṭa vēṭarkaḷ puḷnimittam colli yavaṉi vitukoṇṭē tākkutali valuppaṭuttuvataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka utayaṇaṉi mika aṇmiyil neruṅki vaḷittukkoḷḷa ārampittaṉar.





﻿19. vēṭar kēṭukaḷ

maṟṟavarkaḷ taṉṉit tiṭṭuvatiliruntu tappa oru vaḻi kaṇṭa nimittikaṉ, atik kūṟiṉāṉ. mika nerukkamāka vaḷittukkoṇṭu tākkiyataṉāl, ilavam putarukkuḷḷē maṟintu irunta tattiyum kāñcaṉiyum vēṭarkaḷ kaṇkaḷukkut terintuviṭṭaṉar. appaṭit terintu koṇṭavarkaḷil anta mutiya nimittikaṉum oruvaṉ. “putarukkut tīmūṭṭi viṭṭāl ivaṉ pōri niṟutti viṭuvāṉ” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉic cuṭṭik kāṭṭik kūṟiṉāṉ anta mutiya nimittikaṉ. taṉṉi vitavarkaḷiṭamiruntu tappa inta upāyamtāṉ avaṉukku vāyttatu. anta muṭivi yāvarum ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.

putarukkut tī vikkumāṟu kūṟiya nimittikaṉ kūṟṟi, vēṭar yāvarum varavēṟṟaṉar. āyiṉum nimittikaṉ ataṉi viḷaṅkak kūṟukiṉṟavari avarkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭārkaḷilli. ēṟkeṉavē utayaṇaṉ taṅkaḷi ampukaḷāl vāṭṭiyataṉi niṉikka niṉikka anta nimittikaṉmēl avarkaḷukkuc caṟṟē veṟuppuṇarcci tōṉṟiyiruntatu. nimittikaṉ taṉ kūṟṟi vivarikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. “ilavam putaric cuṟṟit tī mūṭṭiviṭṭāl, uḷḷiruppavarkaḷ evvāṟēṉum veḷippaṭṭuttāṉ ākavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuṅkāl nām avarkaḷic cūḻntu koṇṭu tuṉpuṟutta muṭiyum” eṉṟu kūṟiya piṉpē, nimittikaṉ collil avarkaḷukku nampikki piṟantatu. kiyilirunta araṇik kōlkaḷik kaṭintu tī mūṭṭa muṟpaṭṭaṉar kāṭṭu vēṭar. kāyntu pañcu pōlirunta pūḷip pūkkaḷi iṭimaṭuttu, araṇik kōlkaḷi uracip paḻakkamuḷḷa avarkaḷ tī uṇṭākka veku nēramāka villi. “eṉ puḷ nimittam oru pōtum poyyākātu” eṉṟu ciṟitu tuṇivuṭaṉ nimittikaṉ ippōtu vāyviṭṭuc coṉṉāṉ. mūṭṭiya tīyip putaric cuṟṟilum iṭṭaṉar vēṭar. ‘taṅkaḷi vēṇṭic caraṇaṭiya vēṇṭum; allatu vitavaṇṇam uyirviṭa vēṇṭum. ivviraṇṭoḻiyap putariluḷḷōr vēṟetuvum ceyvataṟkiyalātu’ eṉṟu niṉittavāṟē ciṅkatti vaḷikkum ciṟu narik kūṭṭam pōlap putari vaḷittukkoṇṭu vēṭar tuṉpuṟuttalāyiṉar. tīp puki cūḻntu putariṉuḷḷē mūccuviṭavum iyalātu pōyiṟṟu. taḻittuk koḻittup pūttu viḷaṅkiya pūmputar pukippaṭalaṅkaḷil mella mella maṟintu koṇṭiruntatu. uḷḷē tatti kāṭṭut tīyiṉāl eḻunta pukiyil akappaṭṭukkoṇṭu tikkumukkāṭum peṇ māṉip pōla varuntiṉāḷ. utayaṇaṉiṉ taṉimi avaḷ uḷḷattic cuṭṭatu. taṉporuṭṭu avaṉ paṭum tuyarkaḷiṉ mikutiyi avaḷāl niṉikkavum muṭiyavilli. uḷḷē tuyaralikaḷ poṅki mōtikkoṇṭiruntaṉa. avaḷ peṇmi avvaḷavu tuṉpa mikutiyit tāṅkum aṉupavattikkūṭap peṟṟirukkavilli. tattiyiṉ niliyi avaḷ kūṟāmalē uṇarntukoṇṭa utayaṇaṉ, avaḷarukil vantu aviḻntukiṭanta kūntali mellak kōtiyavāṟu tiruttiṉāṉ. avaḷ tuyar taṇiyac cila kūṟik kāñcaṉa mālikku oru poṟuppi aḷittāṉ. “yāṉ veḷip puṟañ ceṉṟu pōrceytu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭu varukiṟēṉ. ataṟkuḷ nīṅkaḷ iruvarum tīp paṟṟāta oru puṟamāka iṅkiruntu veḷiyēṟic ceṉṟu eṅkēṉum oriṭattil oḷintiruṅkaḷ. ivarkaḷi veṟṟi koṇṭa piṟaku yāṉum nīṅkaḷirukkumiṭam vantu cērntu koḷvēṉ” eṉṟu urittut tattiyik kāñcaṉamāliyiṭam aṭikkalam pōla aḷittāṉ. āṉāl inta āṇiyik kāñcaṉikku iṭṭuviṭṭu iruvariṭamum viṭi peṟṟuk koṇṭu veḷippōnta utayaṇaṉ, itaṟku avaciyamē illātu pōyiṉati uṇarntāṉ. avaṉ villum kiyumāka veḷivanta vēkattik kaṇṇuṟṟa vēṭar iṉam, yāḷiyik kaṇṭa yāṉi iṉampōlac citaṟi ōṭiviṭṭatu. eṉavē, utayaṇaṉ taṉ tiṭṭatti māṟṟik koṇṭāṉ. virivāka uḷḷē ceṉṟu tīp paṟṟāta oru ciṟu muṭukkiṉ vaḻiyē puṟappaṭṭuk koṇṭirunta tattiyiyum kāñcaṉiyiyum tāṉē aḻittukkoṇṭu putariṉ vāyiṟ puṟamākavē veḷiyēṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷuṭaṉ avaṉ putarukku veḷiyē ciṟitu tolivutāṉ naṭantiruppāṉ. tiṭumeṉṟu muṉṉēṟpāṭṭuṭaṉ patuṅkiyiruntu tantiramākac cūḻvatupōl vantu vaḷittatu vēṭar paṭi. appōtu tāṉ utayaṇaṉukku avarkaḷ cūḻcci naṉku purintatu. putariliruntu taṉṉi veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkākavē avarkaḷ añciyatupōla naṭittu vilakiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avaṉ uṇarntāṉ. muṉṉum piṉṉum pakkamum eṅkumē neruṅki vaḷittirunta paṭiyiyum taṉ villiyum māṟimāṟip pārttāṉ avaṉ. utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu pārtta kuṟippip purintukoṇṭa vēṭarkaḷil oruvaṉ mikka matiyuḷḷavaṉ. uṭaṉē taṉ villil ampu toṭuttuc cariyākak kuṟi vittu utayaṇaṉ villiṉ nāṇi aṟuttu iraṇṭākat tuṇṭittuviṭṭāṉ. kalivallunar palar tam kittiṟam viḷaṅkac camitta anta valiya vil, nāṇ aṟuntu toṅkiyatu. camayamaṟintu ceyta anta vēṭaṉiṉ ceyal utayaṇaṉit tikikkac ceytatu. mēlē eṉṉa ceyvateṉṟu puriyātu tikippuṭaṉ niṉṟukoṇṭiruntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. valiyil vīḻntu kaṭṭuṇṭa ciṅkampōla iruntatu avaṉ nili. pāratap pōril apimaṉyuvip pōlat taṉiyāka niṉṟu, taṉtuyar niṉintiruntāṉ avaṉ.

tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarcciyāka neruṅki varumpōtu āṇmiyāḷaṉukku or acātāraṇamāṉatuṇivum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. cuṟṟi niṟpavarkaḷō īvirakkamaṟṟa pakivarkaḷ. kiyil iruppatō nāṇ aṟuntapōṉa veṟum vil taṇṭu. accamum viyappum tōṉṟa iṉam puriyāta tuyarattuṭaṉ tattiyum kāñcaṉiyum aṇittē niṉṟaṉar. ‘kiyil paṭiyētum aṟṟavaṉ’ eṉṟa pōr aṟamum karutātu kaṇikaḷit toṭuttavaṇṇamiruntaṉar vēṭar. veṟṟu villoṉṟē tuṇiyāka, avarkaḷ kaṇi māriyit taṉṉiliruntu ciṟitu nēram vilakkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. avaṉuṭiya inta niliyik kaṇṭa tatti, taṉ maṉattil tuyarat tuṭippuṭaṉ uḷḷaṅkavarnta kaḷvaṉ uṭalit tuḷikkumō eṉṟu añcumpaṭi vēṭar ampukaḷāl tākkukiṉṟa niliyip poṟuttuk koḷḷa muṭiyāmal tavittāḷ. ētō niṉivu vantavaḷpōlat taṉ muṉ kikaḷiyum kaḻuttiyum pārttāḷ. avaḷ mukattil nampikki ciṟitu malarntatu. tāṉ aṇintirunta nakikaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaḻaṟṟiṉāḷ. kāñcaṉiyi arukilaḻittu avaṟṟi utayaṇaṉ mūlam vēṭarkaṭku aḷittu avarkaḷuṭiya pōri niṟuttumāṟu vēṇṭikkoḷḷac coṉṉāḷ. kāñcaṉi caṟṟut tayaṅkiyapiṉ maṟumoḻi kūṟāmal avaṟṟi vāṅkicceṉṟu utayaṇaṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttāḷ.

kāñcaṉi vili matippumikka aṇikalaṉkaḷi utayaṇaṉ kiyiṟ koṭuppatiyum avaṉ oṉṟum puriyātu tikittut tattiyirunta pakkam tirumpi nōkkuvatiyum kaṇṭa vēṭar, aṇikaḷukku ācippaṭṭup pōric ciṟitu taḷarttiṉar. tattiyiṉ naṭukkamum tuyara maṉaniliyum utayaṇaṉukku mika viriviṟ pulappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. noṭikku noṭi tuṉpam mikuntu nerukkumpōtu, niṉivukku aḷavu kaṭanta nuṇmiyum vēkamum kūrmiyum eṅkiruntō kiṭittu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. utayaṇaṉ niṉivil ciṟiyatōr cūḻcci mika virivil uruvāki viṭṭatu. accūḻcciyiṉ tiṭṭappaṭi naṭakka avaṉ tayārāyiṉaṉ. tāṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉpati uriyāmal vēṭarkaḷi nōkkik kūṟalāṉāṉ. “kāṭṭu muḻikaḷiluṟiyum valiya tōḷiyuṭiya vēṭarkaḷē, caṟṟu arukē vantu yāṉ kūṟap pōvatik kēḷuṅkaḷ. pala periya aṇikalap poruḷkaḷi muyaṟciyāl īṭṭip piṭimītu koṇṭu, iv vaḻiyē vanta yāṅkaḷ vāṇikarkaḷ. varumpōtu ivviṭattiṟkuc ciṟitu tolivil eṅkaḷ piṭi nōyāl vīḻntu iṟantu viṭṭatu. piṉṉar yāṅkaḷ mikavum maṉaṅkavaṉṟu vaḻiyiliruntu ciṟitu tolivu ceṉṟu īṭṭivanta perum poruḷkaḷiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum ōriṭattil putittu vittirukkiṟōm. nīṅkaḷ pōri niṟuttuvīrkaḷāyiṉ poruḷip putitta iṭatti uṅkaṭkuk kāṭṭukiṉṟōm. avikaḷi nīṅkaḷē aṭiyalām” eṉṟu kūṟi muṭittāṉ.

utayaṇaṉ kūṟṟic ceviyuṟṟa vēṭar pōri niṟuttiṉar. cuṟṟi vaḷittirunta vēṭarkaḷi vilakkiviṭṭu avarkaḷ talivaṉ muṉvantāṉ. utayaṇaṉi neruṅkiya vēṭar talivaṉ, “nī yār eṉpati emakku viḷakkamākak kūṟavēṇṭum” eṉṟu miraṭṭiṉāṉ. inta viṉāvik kēṭṭa utayaṇaṉ oru kaṇam tikippu aṭintāṉ. āṉāl uṟutiyākak kaṭippiṭittāl oḻiyat taṉ cūḻcci uṭaṉē veṟṟi peṟāteṉpati uṇarntu, “yām utayaṇaṉuṭiya vāṇikar. perum poruḷuṭaṉ piṭiyil vantōm. iṭiyil piṭi vīḻntuviṭṭatu. poruḷi vaḻikku appāl oru poḻilil putittu vittōm” eṉṟu muṉ coṉṉatiyē maṟupaṭiyum poy kalantu viḷakkamāka urittāṉ. taṉatu inta viṭiyil vēṭar talivaṉukku makiḻcci ēṟpaṭṭati avaṉ mukak kuṟippiliruntu utayaṇaṉ aṟintu koṇṭāṉ. tatti kaḻaṟṟiyaḷitta nakikaḷiyum avarkaḷukku aḷittāṉilli utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉuṭiya paṭikaḷuṭaṉ tuṇikku vantu cērumvari taṉakkum tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷukkum vēṭarkaḷāl peruntuṉpam nērātavāṟu pēcciṉālēyē taṭuttuk koḷḷavē utayaṇaṉ ivvāṟu oru muḻup poyyic colla nērntatu. atu karuti utayaṇaṉ ceyta ic cūḻcci takka payaṉi aḷittatu. ‘avarkaḷ vattava nāṭṭu maṉṉaṉ utayaṇaṉiṉ vaṇikarkaḷ’ eṉṟa kūṟṟik kēṭṭuc caṟṟu mariyāti koṇṭu, vēṭarkaḷ tuṉpuṟuttuvati muṟṟilum nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum putittu vittuḷḷa poruṭkuviyi evvāṟēṉum paṟittuk koḷḷavē virumpiṉar. paṟittuk koḷḷa muṭiyum eṉṟa nampikkiyil ciṟitu makiḻcciyum aṭintaṉar. vēṭar talivaṉ, utayaṇaṉi neruṅki avaṉ mēlāṭiyālēyē avaṉuṭiya iru kikaḷiyum piṇittup “perum poruḷip putitta iṭattik kāṭṭuka” eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. taṅkaḷiṭam utayaṇaṉ caraṇaṭintu viṭṭamikku aṟikuṟiyākavē mēlāṭiyāl utayaṇaṉip piṇittāṉ eyiṉar talivaṉ. utayaṇaṉum taṉ cūḻcci veṟṟiyuṟum eṉṟa nampikkiyil ataṉip poṟuttuk koṇṭāṉ. utayaṇaṉ meḷaṉamāyirukkavē nakikaḷip putitta iṭattik kāṭṭumāṟu mīṇṭum avaṉit tūṇṭiṉāṉ vēṭar talivaṉ. utayaṇaṉ talinimirntu avaṉip pārttāṉ. “nīṅkaḷ iṭṭa neruppiṉ naṭuvē iruntatāl mikavum varuntiyuḷḷōm. uṭalmuḻutum kāntukiṟatu. nāṅkaḷ muṉpu taṅkiyirunta poḻilil ētō oru pakutiyilē poruḷkaḷip putittōm. tīyāl citivupaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟa iṅkē, ippōtu ap pakuti etu eṉṟu kuṟippākat teriyavilli. ituvum uṅkaḷ tīyiṉāl vanta viṉitāṉ. eṉavē anta neruppiṉ vēkam caṟṟut taṇintu āṟaṭṭum. āṟiyapiṉ nāṅkaḷ putitta iṭattik kāṭṭukiṟēṉ. atuvari poṟuttiruṅkaḷ” eṉṟu taṉṉik kūrntu nōkkum vēṭar talivaṉiṉ, kaḻukuk kaṇkaḷi ūṭuruviyavāṟē talinimirntu maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭa vēṭar talivaṉ “aḻal āṟiyapiṉ poruḷ putitta iṭatti nī kāṭṭavilliyāyiṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa uṉ karaṅkaḷ veṭṭappeṟuvatu uṟuti” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉiṭam kaṭumiyāka moḻintāṉ.

vēṭar talivaṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiyatik kēṭṭa tatti, tīyil viḻunta iḷantaḷireṉa maṇavāṭṭaṅ koṇṭu urukiṉāḷ. utayaṇaṉ tuyarcūḻa niṟkiṟāṉē eṉṟa niṉivil kaṇṇirit tuḷittaṉa avaḷ kayal viḻikaḷ. kāñcaṉi, tattiyit taḻuvik koṇṭavāṟē, “utayaṇaṉ tuyari niṉ tuyarāka eṇṇupavaḷ nī! avaṉuṭiya uyirkku ūṟu variṉ nī iṟattal orutali. tantiyiyum avaṉ peruñ celvattiyum nīkkik kātalaṉip piṉpaṟṟi vanta uṉṉi viti ippaṭittāṉ naṭattum pōlum” eṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟiṉāḷ. ivarkaḷ ivvāṟu tuyaril aḻuntuvatik kaṇṭa utayaṇaṉ ivarkaḷukku āṟutal kūṟa virumpiṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ vēṭar talivaṉiyum maṟṟavarkaḷiyum nōkki, “uṅkaḷ viruppattukku māṟākāmal varuttantavirntu yāṅkaḷ putitta poruḷkaḷik kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉāl ippōtikku eṉ kikkaṭṭi aviḻttu viṭuṅkaḷ. piṉpu vēṇṭumāṉāl mīṇṭum kaṭṭik koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atil eṉakku maṟuppilli. ivaḷuṭiya tuyarattit tēṟṟiya piṉ, tīppuki āṟiyatum poruḷkaḷip putitta iṭattik kāṭṭuvēṉ” eṉṟaṉaṉ. atik kēṭṭa vēṭarkaḷ mutalil maṟuppatupōṉṟu kaṭumiyāka naṭantu koṇṭālum iṟutiyil ‘ivaṉ nam kiyiṟ ciṟip paṭṭavaṉ. poy collit tappa eṇṇi ivvāṟu kūṟiyiruppāṉāyiṉ ivaṉi uyirōṭu viṭamāṭṭōm. eṉavē ippōtikku ivaṉ colvati yellām ceytutāṉ vippōmē’ eṉṟu karutik kikkaṭṭi aviḻttuviṭṭaṉar. valiyiliruntu viṭupaṭṭup piṇiyi nōkki oṭum kalimāṉip pōlat tattiyi nōkkic ceṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





20. paṭi vantatu!

araṇmaṉip pañcaṇikaḷil pūvaṉiya mañcaṅkaḷilē tuyiṉṟa vācavatatti, aṉṟu aṅkē antap pāli maṇalilē taḷarntu cōrnta vaṇṇam kāñcaṉiyiṉ maṭiyil paṭuttiruntatu utayaṇaṉi eṉṉavō ceytatu. vēṭarkaḷiṭamiruntu utayaṇaṉ kikkaṭṭi aviḻttuk koṇṭu taṉṉi nōkki varuvatukaṇṭa tatti eḻuntāḷ. kāñcaṉi vilaki niṉṟu koṇṭāḷ. tīp pukiyiṉālum naṭanta iḷippiṉālum vāṭiyirunta tattiyiṉ mukattil nāṇaṅ kalanta malarcci piṟantatu. avaḷit taṉ nīṇṭa kikaḷāl mella taḻuvikkoṇṭē āṟutal kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. kuvaḷi malarkaḷiliruntu muttutirvatu pōlat tattiyiṉ kaṇkaḷiliruntu corinta nīr muttukkaḷ, utayaṇaṉit tīṇṭiyatāl ēṟpaṭṭa makiḻcciyiṉ viḷivākat tāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. eppōtum mikunta tuṉpattiṟku iṭiyil tōṉṟum miṉṉal nēra makiḻcciyiltāṉ iṉpam mikuti. kikkeṭṭukiṉṟa mallikippūvik kāṭṭilum naccup pāmpukaḷ cūḻntu vacikkum iṭattiluḷḷa maṉōrañcita malarukku maṇam atikamallavā? iṅkē ivarkaḷ nili ivvāṟirukkap puṭpaka nakaram ceṉṟa vayantakaṉ niliyi aṟiya avaṉip piṉpaṟṟuvōm.

‘āruyir naṇpaṉum aracakumāraṉumākiya utayaṇaṉit tuṉpattiṟkuriya cūḻniliyil taṉittirukkac ceytuviṭṭu vantirukkiṟōmē' eṉṟa kavali orupuṟam. etirē mikkuḻampeṉak kuvintukiṭanta iruḷil puṭpaka nakarattiṉ pāti teriyātu pōyiṉa tuyaram orupuṟam. virivil iṭavakaṉic cantittup paṭiyuṭaṉ tirumpa vēṇṭumeṉṟa paraparappu orupuṟam. ivvaḷavum cēra iruḷik kiḻittu oṭum miṉṉalpōl muṉṉēṟic ceṉṟāṉ vayantakaṉ. etirē puṭpaka nakarattiṉ māṭamāḷikikaḷil oḷi ceyta viḷakkukaḷum piṟavum taṉṉuṭiya kaṇṇukkut terikiṉṟa aḷavu nakari neruṅkiya piṟakutāṉ vayantakaṉukku uyir vantatu. appōtutāṉ pūtta mallikipōlak kīḻ vāṉilum karumi nīṅki veḷittu utayamākak koṇṭiruntatu. vayantakaṉ puṭpaka nakaruḷ nuḻintu araṇmaṉiyiṉ koṭimatiṟ puṟatti aṭintāṉ. kāvalar aṉumati peṟṟu iṭavakaṉik kāṇa uḷḷē ceṉṟa vayantakaṉ. oṟṟaṉ oruvaṉiṭam ētō taṉittup pēcik koṇṭirunta niliyil aṅkē iṭavakaṉik kaṇṭāṉ. vayantakaṉik kaṇṭa iṭavakaṉ, oṟṟaṉi appaṭiyē viṭṭu viṭṭu āvalōṭu muṉvantu varavēṟṟāṉ. taṉṉi varavēṟṟa iṭavakaṉ mukattil tōṉṟiya kalakkamum tuyaramum, etu kāraṇamāka viḷintavi eṉṟu vayantakaṉukkup pulappaṭa villi. utayaṇaṉuṭiya nalatti vicāritta iṭavakaṉ mukattil paraparapput tōṉṟiyatum, utayaṇaṉ nalameṉpati aṟintavuṭaṉ kalakalaveṉṟu vāyviṭṭuc cirittatum vayantakaṉukkup putirākavē iruntaṉa. antap putiri iṭavakaṉ aviḻttapōtu vayantakaṉum avaṉōṭu cērntu cirikka nērntatu. ‘utayaṇaṉuṭiya ūkkam, uyarcci, oḻukkam, kalinalam ivikaḷi ellām kaṇṭu poṟāmi koṇṭa piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, uḷḷē īṭṭi, vēl mutaliya āyutaṅkaḷik kuttiṭṭu niṟuttiya poynnilam oṉṟi amittu, vañcakamāka atilē vīḻtti utayaṇaṉik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu kāṭṭu vēṭar mūlamākat tāṉ kēḷviyuṟṟatākavum ac ceytiyē taṉ tuyarattukkum paraparappiṟkum kāraṇameṉṟum iṭavakaṉ coṉṉapōtu, vayantakaṉ vāyviṭṭuc cirittāṉ. poy collukiṟavarkaḷ evvaḷavu aḻakāka ataṟkuk ki kālkaḷi oṭṭa vittu uṇmiyippōla uruvākki viṭukiṟārkaḷ eṉpati niṉikkumpōtu vayantakaṉukku viyappāyiruntatu. taṉatu kāriyattiṉ turitatti niṉiviṟ koṇṭu vantavaṉāy, utayaṇaṉuṭiya appōtiya niliyi avantiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭatu toṭaṅki ilavam putaril taṅkiyiruppatuvari virivākak kūṟi, uṭaṉē paṭiyutavi tēvippaṭuvatiyum viḷakkiṉāṉ vayantakaṉ. atōṭu taṉṉi nampuvataṟkāka utayaṇaṉ kūṟiya aṭiyāḷa moḻiyiyum vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉiṭam aṟivittāṉ. iṭavakaṉ uṭaṉē taṉ paṭikaḷip puṟappaṭumāṟu āṇiyiṭṭāṉ. paṭikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. iṭavakaṉum vayantakaṉum muṉceṉṟaṉar. paṭi piṉ toṭarntatu, iṭavakaṉ āṇippaṭi paṭikaḷukkuppiṉ vācavatattikkum utayaṇaṉukkum uriyavākap palavaki alaṅkārap poruḷkaḷ cumantu koṇṭu varappeṟṟaṉa. aṇikalaṉkaḷum parivāramum paṇip peṇkaḷumāka ak kūṭṭam paṭikkuppiṉ amitiyākac ceṉṟatu.

paṭi kāṭṭuḷḷē vantuviṭṭatu. muṉ ceṉṟa vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum ilavamputari aṭintaṉar. aṅkē avarkaḷukkup periya ēmāṟṟam kāttiruntatu. putar eriyum putiyumākat tīppaṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu putarukku etirē utayaṇiṉ ampukku ilakkākiya vēṭarkaḷ cintiya kurutiyum niṉamum parantiruntaṉa. mēlē anta niṇavirunti etirnōkkik kaḻukukaḷum kākkikaḷum vaṭṭa miṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉa. erintu koṇṭirunta ilavam putarum etirē terinta kurutip parappum kaṇṭa avarkaḷ utayaṇaṉukkum kāṭṭiluḷḷa vēṭarkaḷukkum ētō pōr naṭantirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu uyttuṇarntaṉar. āṉāl utayaṇaṉum tatti, kāñcaṉi ākiyōrkaḷum taṅkiyirunta ilavam putar tīppaṭṭu erintu koṇṭiruntatutāṉ vayantakaṉ aiyappaṭak kāraṇamākiyatu. ‘oruvēḷi utayaṇaṉ tatti mutaliyavarkaḷ neruppil aḻintu pōyirukkak kūṭumō?’ eṉpataṟkumēl vayantakaṉāl niṉikkavē muṭiyavilli. avaṉ vāyviṭṭu aḻutē viṭṭāṉ. mēlē eṉṉa ceyvateṉṟu aṟiyāmal avarkaḷ aṅkēyē ayarntupōy amarntu viṭṭaṉar. utayaṇaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷukku ētēṉum tuyarnērntiruntāl tāṅkaḷum naṭpi nilināṭṭa uyir viṭuvatākavē muṭivu ceytaṉar. iṭavakaṉum vayantakaṉum. itaṟkuḷ piṉ toṭarnta paṭiyum aṅkē vantu cērntatu. peruntuyaruṭaṉ amarntirunta taṅkaḷ aracaṉiyum vayantakaṉiyum kaṇṇuṟṟa paṭit talivarkaḷ varuntat takka ētō oṉṟu nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṉumāṉittuk koṇṭaṉar. vayantakaṉ vāyilāka naṭantavaṟṟi aṟintapiṉ avarkaḷ “tāṅkaḷ karutuvatupōla utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōrukkut tuyaram etuvum nērntirakkātu. itō puṟparappiṉ iṭiyē teriyum aṭiccuvaṭukaḷ vēṭarkaḷuṭiyaṉa. ic cuvaṭikaḷip piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟāl utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōric cantittālum cantikkalām” eṉṟu uṟutikūṟi, iṭavakaṉukkut tiriya mūṭṭiṉar. paṭit talivar kūṟṟil caṟṟē nampikki varappeṟṟavarāy vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum eḻuntu anta aṭiccuvaṭukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē piṉpaṟṟi naṭantaṉar. ēṉiyōrum piṉ toṭarntaṉar. caṟṟut tolivu ceṉṟatum paṭittalivaṉ malic cariviṉ kīḻē ciṟuciṟu uruvilē eṟumpukkūṭṭam pōlat tōṉṟiya vēṭar kūṭṭam pulveḷiyil naṭuvē terintatu. antak kūṭṭattip paṭiyuṭaṉ neruṅkiya avarkaḷ, vēṭarkaḷukku iṭiyē utayaṇaṉ, tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷuṭaṉ niṟpatik kaṇṭu aḷavilā makiḻcci aṭintaṉar. -

iṭavakaṉuṭiya paṭikaḷ vēṭari vaḷittuk koṇṭaṉa. cuṟṟik karuṅkuvaḷi malar pūtta poykiyuḷ naṭu miyattil orē oru tāmari pūttāṟ pōla niṉṟāṉ utayaṇaṉ. cuṟṟi vaḷittut tuṉpuṟuttik koṇṭirunta vēṭarkaḷ atircci aṭintaṉar. pakkattil karuṅkāli marattiṉ mēlirunta paṟaviyoṉṟu orumuṟi kattiyatu. atik kēṭṭa nimittikaṉ, “iṉi namakkut tuṉpam nērum. nām ivviṭatti viṭṭu ōṭiviṭutal nallatu” eṉṟu vēṭarkaḷi nōkkik kūṟiṉāṉ. avarkaḷ yāvarum avaṉi veṟuppuṭaṉ erittuviṭuvatu pōlap pārttaṉar. nāṟpuṟamum citaṟi ōṭuvataṟkut toṭaṅkiya vēṭarkaḷ. iṟuti muṟiyāka utayaṇaṉi neruṅkit tuṉpuṟuttalāyiṉar. atik kaṇṭa iṭavakaṉ paṭiyiṉar, virivil vantu vēṭarkaḷi villum vāḷum vēlum koṇṭu etirttaṉar. itu utayaṇaṉakku ēṟpaṭuttiya nili, irutalik koḷḷi pōla iruntatu. ip puṟam vēṭarkaḷ tuyaram poṟukka muṭiyavilli. vantirukkum paṭiyiṉar taṉakku vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu terintuviṭṭāl, pōkiṉṟa pōkkil ciṉantīra ētāvatu ceytuviṭa vēṭarkaḷ tayaṅka māṭṭārkaḷ. itaṟkāka vantiruppavar evareṉpatiyē aṟintu koḷḷātavaṉpōla utayaṇaṉ naṭikka nērntatu. “vantirukkiṉṟa paṭiyiṉar uṅkaḷic cērntavarkaḷā? piṟarā? ivarkaḷāl namakku ētēṉum tuṉpam nērumāyiṉ eṅkaḷi iṅkē eṅkāvatu maṟintirukkac ceyyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟu vēṭarkaḷi nōkkik kūṟi ēmāṟṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

ataṟku vēṭarkaḷ “itu utayaṇaṉiṉ mantirikaḷākiya iṭavakaṉ paṭi. uyirtappa virumpiṉāyāyiṉ eṅkaḷōṭu ōṭivaruka” eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟiviṭṭut tāṅkaḷ tappa vaḻitēṭi, virumpiya ticikaḷil oṭalāyiṉar. cilar puṟputarkaḷil oḷintu ōṭiṉar. patukkik kaṟkaḷiṉ iṭiyilē patuṅkiyavāṟu virintaṉar vēṟu cilar. eñciyavarkaḷil iṭavakaṉ paṭivīraruṭiya vāḷukku iriyāyiṉar cilar. paṭivīrar viyakka, vēṭṭuvap pōrttiṟaṅ kāṭṭip pōriṭṭaṉar cilar. vayantakaṉum iṭavakaṉum kūṭap pōril īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar pōlum. vēṭṭuvarkaḷil perumpālōr ōṭi viṭṭārkaḷēṉum, eñciyavarāy niṉṟu pōriṭṭa cilarukkē iṭavakaṉ paṭi vīrarkaḷ muṟṟilum muyaṉṟu viṭikūṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. in niliyil utayaṇaṉ, tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷuṭaṉē ciṟitu vilaki otukkup puṟamāka ōriṭattil maṟintirukka virumpiṉāṉ. ataṟku vēṟōr kāraṇamum iruntatu. tāṉum tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷum irunta iṭam iraṇṭu tarattup paṭiyiṉarukkum iṭiyil amintiruntatu. ‘avarkaḷukkuḷ parimāṟik koḷḷum ampukaḷ vēlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭiyilirukkum taṅkaḷukku ētēṉum ūṟu ceytalum kūṭum’ eṉṟu karutip pakkattil maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu vaḷarntirunta oriṭattil tatti, kāñcaṉi ivarkaḷuṭaṉ maṟivākac ceṉṟu utayaṇaṉ iruntu koṇṭāṉ.

pōr oru vaḻiyāka muṭintatu. cīṟi eḻunta pāmpiṉ muṉ elikkaṇampōla maṟinta cuvaṭu teriyātapaṭi āki viṭṭatu vēṭar paṭi. utayaṇaṉ veḷiyē vantāṉ. vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ mutaliyōrum paṭit talivarkaḷum aḷappariya maṉamakiḻcciyuṭaṉ tiṅkaḷic cūḻnta viṇ mīṉiṉampōla utayaṇaṉic cūḻntukoṇṭu veṟṟik kaḷippu viḷaṅka āravāram ceytaṉar. pirinta naṇparkaḷ kūṭiṉar. pēcariya maṉaniṟivi aṭintaṉar. iṭavakaṉ, utayaṇaṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu kaṇṇirviṭṭa kāṭci, kūṭiyiruntavarkaḷi urukkiyatu. allal akaṉṟa makiḻcciyil tattiyum kāñcaṉiyum veḷivantu orupuṟamāka niṉṟu koṇṭaṉar. aṭukkaṭukkāka eḻuntu vanta tuṉpaṅkaḷi ariya tuṇiyāka niṉṟu pōkkiya vayantakaṉ ippōtu iṭavakaṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉ takka taruṇattil vantu utaviyirā viṭṭāl taṉkati eṉṉa ākum?’ eṉṟu ceṉṟa utayaṇaṉ maṉa eṇṇaṅkaḷ caṭṭeṉṟu taṭippaṭṭaṉa. etirē vayantakaṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. appōtu avaṉi utayaṇaṉ pārtta kaṉinta pārviyil naṉṟiyaṟivu pūraṇamākak kaṉintu terintatu.

appōtikkup pakkattilirunta ciṟu cōli oṉṟil yāvarum taṅkiṉar. veku nāḷkaḷukkup piṟaku mīṇṭum cantikkiṉṟārkaḷākiyāl iṭavakaṉum utayaṇaṉum iṭi viṭātu pēcikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. vayantakaṉ pakkattil amarntiruntāṉ. tuṉpap peruṅkaṭalik kaṭakka iṉpap puṇiyāka vantutaviṉarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷiṭam mīṇṭum nā taḻutaḻukka naṉṟi kūṟiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ.





﻿21. maliccāralilē

cōliyil taṅki utayaṇaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ aḷavaḷāvik koṇṭiruntapōtu iṭavakaṉiṉ paṭivīrarkaḷ pakkattilē irunta cayanti nakarattic cārnta maliccāralil, utayaṇaṉ tatti mutaliyōr taṅkuvataṟkēṟṟa paṭa māṭaṅkaḷiyum ēṉiyōrkkuriya paṭivīṭukaḷiyum amittuk koṇṭiruntaṉar. maṟukum muṟṟamum muṉṟilumāka vakuttu amikkappaṭṭirunta antap pāṭi vīṭukaḷiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷ maliccāralil taṉi aḻakuṭaṉē tōṉṟiṉa. tūyaveṇ paṭṭukkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa paṭa māṭaṅkaḷ toliviliruntu kāṇpataṟku vicumpilē kaṭṭiya kaṇṇāṭi nakar pōlat terintaṉa. vācavatattikku iyaṟṟiya pāṭi vīṭṭil paḷḷimāṭam, aṉṟilum viḷiyāṭu muṉṟil mutaliyavaṟṟi aḻakuṟac camittiruntaṉar. cantaṉap palaki, maṇikkalap pēḻi irukkik kaṭṭil, ālavaṭṭam mutaliya alaṅkārap poruḷ atil nirampiyiruntaṉa. utayaṇaṉukkuriya paṭamāṭak kōvililum oru ciṟanta aracaṉukku vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum poruḷkaḷum mikuntirukkumāṟu iyaṟṟappaṭṭiruntatu. palavakik kuṟṟiḷiyōr āṭavarum makaḷirumākap paṇi ceyyak kāttiruntaṉar. oruvarumē aṟṟa cūṉiyappiratēcamākiya antak kāṭṭil, maliccāralil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nakaril ivvaḷavu pēr tōṉṟiyatu puṟṟiliruntu īcal puṟappaṭṭatu pōliruntatu. nalla nēram pārttu utayaṇaṉ mutaliya yāvarum cōli nīṅki maliccāralil amikkap peṟṟa nakaril kuṭipukuntaṉar. peṇyāṉiyiṉmītu vēkamāka vanta uṭal nōvutīra maruttuva muṟippaṭi uṇavu camikkap paṭṭatu. nāḻikik kaṇakkar uṇṇa vēṇṭiya poḻutaṟintu kūṟiṉar. iravum pakalumāka viṭātu payaṇam ceyta aluppu utayaṇaṉic cōrvu koḷḷac ceytiruntatu. vēṭarkaḷōṭu pōr ceyta kaḷippu vēṟu avaṉi varuttiyatu. uṭal aluppu nīṅka eṇṇey pūci nīrāṭiviṭṭup piṉpu uṇṇalām eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. uṭal oḷikuṟintu vāṭṭaṅ kaṇṭiruntatu. vallavaṉ vakutta vācaṉi poruntiya eṇṇeyip pūcikkoṇṭu kuḷirnta nīril maṉam virumpi veku nēram uṭal kuḷira āṭiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. nīrāṭi muṭintapiṉ naṇparkaḷ oṉṟāka uṇṇa amarntaṉar. veku nāḷkaḷukkup piṟaku nērnta inta ariya amitiyāṉa vāyppu mūvar maṉattilum makiḻcciyi niṟiyac ceytiruntatu. makiḻcci niṟivōṭu utayaṇaṉ, vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ mūvarum uṇṭaṉar. cirippum viḷiyāṭṭumāka uṇṭu muṭikka nēramāyviṭṭālum uvaki mikutiyil poḻutu pōṉatē teriyavilli naṇparkaḷukku. piṭiyil vaḻippayaṇam ceyta iḷippu. uṟakkamiṉṟip pala iravukaḷik kaḻittami, vēṭarkaḷ tolli, tīyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa vētaṉi, ivvaḷavum tīra naṉṟāka ōyvu eṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ utayaṇaṉ.

vācavatattiyi āyamakaḷir naṉku nīrāṭṭiṉar. aḻakiya kaṟcuṉiyiṉ naṭuvē poṟpāvipōla niṉṟu, kāṇpōr makiḻa naṟunīrāṭiṉāḷ tatti. nīrāṭiyapiṉ pacitīra amutam pōṉṟa uṇavi uṇṇac ceytaṉar. iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷāka uṇṇāmaliruntu paci mikuntirunta tatti naṉku uṇṭāḷ. cuvimikkatākac camikkap paṭṭiruntatu anta nalluṇavu. uṇavu muṭintapiṉ tōḻip peṇkaḷ tuyartīrap pala paṇiviṭikaḷ ceytaṉar. yāvarum oyvu koṇṭaṉar. appōtu maliccāralil nakariṉ amitiyāṉa tōṟṟam aḻakiṉ elliyāka viḷaṅkiyatu. paṭiyōṭu vantirunta kutirikaḷ, yāṉikaḷ ivi yāvum periya periya maraṅkaḷilē kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. nakariṉ amitiyik kalittuk koṇṭu yāṉikaḷiṉ muḻakkam maliccāralil kēṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu. pōkapūmiyik kāṭṭilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya antac ceyaṟki nakariṉ nūlveṇ māṭaṅkaḷ pāṟkaṭaliṉ alikaḷip pōlak kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. moyttuk kiṭanta paṭi vīrarkaḷ, kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka oyvupeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.

utayaṇaṉ mutaliyōr iḷippāṟi eḻuntatum pakkattiluḷḷa cayanti nakarattiṟkuc celluvataṟkut tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar. mērumali mēl eḻunta ceṅkatirc celvaṉ pōla aḻakiṟ ciṟanta yāṉi oṉṟiṉmītu ēṟi amarntāṉ utayaṇaṉ. vayantakaṉum iṭivakaṉum pakkattē vēṟu yāṉikaḷiṉmītu ēṟi varalāyiṉar. irupuṟamum yāṉikaḷ cūḻa naṭuvil utayaṇaṉ irunta tōṟṟam, cuṟṟilum karu mukil iruppa naṭuvē cuṭar iruntateṉa viḷaṅkiṟṟu. avaṉ talikkumēlē niḻal ceytu koṇṭirunta, veṇkoṟṟakkuṭi, taṇmati pōlat terintatu.

niri niriyāka villum vāḷum vēlum kēṭayamum ēntip paṭikaḷ piṉceṉṟaṉa. yāṉi puravi tēr mutaliyaṉa variciyākap piṉpaṟṟip pōyiṉa. potiyiṟ cantaṉamum vintiyattu yāṉik kompukaḷum mērumalip poṉṉum kuṭakaṭaliṟ piṟanta paṭarkoṭip pavaḻamum teṉkaṭaṟ piṟanta miṉṉoḷi muttum īḻattuc ceppum imayattu vayiramum ākiya alaṅkārap poruḷ pala ēntiya ēvaliḷiyar avarkaḷukkup piṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. yavaṉattut taccarum avanti nāṭṭuk kollarum makatanāṭṭu maṇiviṉiñarum pāṭali nāṭṭup poṟṟoḻilāḷarum kōcalattu oviyarum vattavanāṭṭu vaṇṇakkammarum ākiya toḻiṟ celvarkaḷiṉ kalik kūṭṭam appāl pōyiṟṟu. kāṭṭil utayaṇaṉukku utavavē iṭavakaṉ vantāṉ eṉiṉum, taṉakkup pēraracaṉākiya utayaṇaṉi avaṉ amiccaruḷ oruvaṉākiya tāṉ kāṇac cellumpōtu takka mariyātikaḷuṭaṉ cella vēṇṭum. aṉṟiyum utayaṇaṉi maṇañ ceytukoḷḷa avaṉ āruyirk kātali tattiyum uṭaṉ vantiruppatāl ēṟṟa alaṅkārap poruḷkaḷuṭaṉum etir koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiyē ivvāṟu pal poruḷum pala viṉiñarum koṇṭu vantiruntāṉ iṭavakaṉ. vācavatatti kollappaṇṭi (oruvaki cittirac civiki) oṉṟil kāñcaṉi tuṇi niṉṟu ēṟṟa ēṟikkoṇṭāḷ. kūṭiyirunta makkaḷ kūṭṭatti vilakkak kikkōṟ cilatar kuraliṭṭa olikaḷukkiṭiyē puṟap pāṭṭukku aṟikuṟiyāṉa muraca oliyum kalantolittatu.

maliccāraliluḷḷa ciṟṟurkaḷil vāḻum kuṟunila makkaḷākiya kuṟumparkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ talivarkaḷum muṉṉarē iṭavakaṉ mūlam utayaṇaṉuṭiya varavu kuṟittu olic ceyti peṟṟiruntaṉar. aṅkaṅkē kūṭi niṉṟu utayaṇaṉuṭiya aṭiyāḷa ilacciṉiyi mariyātiyuṭaṉ kaṇṭuṇarntu palvakit tiṟip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ cantittaṉar. kuṟumpar makkaḷ, avarkaḷ talivar mutaliyōr, yāṉit tattamum malittēṉum pala kaṉivakikaḷum vīṇi ceyvataṟkēṟṟa marat taṇṭukaḷum mūṅkilil viṉinta muttukkaḷum akilum pulittōlum ākiya pala poruḷkaḷi aṉpuṭaṉ kiyuṟiyākak koṭuttaṉar. ‘ekkālattum niṉ kuṭiyiṉarāka vāḻum pēṟu emakkuk kiṭṭuka’ eṉṟa avarkaḷ vēṇṭukōḷi utayaṇaṉ makiḻcciyōṭu varavēṟṟu mikka naṉṟi celuttiyapiṉ, avarkaḷiṭam viṭipeṟṟu mēṟceṉṟāṉ. appāl ivvāṟē pala ciṟuciṟu malikaḷiyum kāṭukaḷiyum ciṟṟuārkaḷiyum kaṭantu payaṇattit toṭarntaṉar. iṭiiṭiyē taṉ kīḻ vāḻum kuṭi makkaḷiṉ aṉpu kaṉinta varavēṟpu utayaṇaṉukku maṉakiḻvi aḷittatu. nikaḻnta tuṉpaṅkaḷi maṟappataṟku itu nalla vāyppākavum amintatu. malip pakutikaḷilum kāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻum am makkaḷ kaḷḷaṅkapaṭam aṟṟavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṉpu vañcaṉi kalavāta tūymi vāyntatu. atil taṅkaḷ aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil matippum kalantiruntatu. poḻutu cāyum nēram. yāvarum cayanti nakaratti neruṅkiṉar. aḻakiṟ ciṟanta cayanti nakaram māli nērattu mañcaḷ veyilil poṉ mayamākak kāṭci aḷittatu. tēvakōṭṭattu maṇiyōci varuvōrellām varuka eṉṟu aḻippatupōl olittuk koṇṭiruntatu. cayanti nakari utayaṇaṉuṭiya amiccarkaḷil oruvaṉākiya urumaṇṇuvā āṇṭu vantāṉ. cayanti nakaram utayaṇaṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭatāyiṉ, avaṉ āṇiyiṉmēl urumaṇṇuvā āṇṭu vantāṉ. cayanti nāṟpuṟamum iyaṟki vaḷamikka malikaḷ cūḻnta nakar. eṉṟum kuṉṟāta eḻilvaḷamum cārntatu.

anta nakarattiṟkuḷ tatti, kāñcaṉi, vayantakaṉ, iṭavakaṉ ivarkaḷ puṭicūḻa mikka viruppattuṭaṉ utayaṇaṉ pukuntaṉaṉ. paṭikaḷ yāvum puṟanakarilē taṅki viṭṭaṉa. utayaṇaṉ varavu muṉpē olic ceyti mūlam aṟivikkappaṭṭiruntataṉāl, nakaril eṅkum kaḷippuṭaṉ kūṭiya āravāram mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭatu. pūraṇa viḷakku, tōraṇa mālikaḷum cuṭaroḷi vitāṉaṅkaḷumākap polivum putumiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa, nakarapperu vītikaḷ yāvum. avaṟṟi makiḻcciyuṭaṉ nōkkiyavāṟē ivarkaḷ ceṉṟaṉar.





22. cayanti nakaril tirumaṇam

katiravaṉuṭiya vemmiyi āṟṟic cayanti nakarukku niḻalaḷippavaṉpōla viritta pūṅkoṭikaḷ pala tōṉṟiṉa. periya muracaṅkaḷiṉ kaṭal pōṉṟa olikku iṭiyē, kuṭamuḻākkaḷiṉ kiṇkiṇi iciyum olittatu. iruviṉip paḻantuyarkaḷi veṉṟa arukatēvaṉukku intira ulakam varavēṟpaḷittatu pōla, utayaṇaṉ tatti mutaliyōri makaḷirum mintarumākak kūṭic cayanti nakarattiṉar maṅkala niṟi kuṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭu varavēṟṟaṉar. iruḷ kaṭintu tōṉṟum iḷañāyiṟu pōlat tuyar kaṭintu poliyum tōṉṟalē varuka eṉṟār cilar. cayanti nakariṉar tam aṉpu niṟivi ellām utayaṇaṉ varavēṟpil kāṭṭiṉar. “yāṉiyi mīṇṭum peṟak kāṉiṟ ceṉṟapōtu paki maṉṉaṉāl paṟṟik koḷḷappaṭṭāy! ippōtu veṟṟiyiyum ataṉ ciṉṉamāka vācavatattiyiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu vantirukkiṟāy! uṉakkup putalvar pōṉṟa uṉ kuṭikaḷi iṉit tuṉpam nīṅka āḷavēṇṭiyatu uṉ kaṭami!” eṉṟu mutumakkaḷ vāḻttiṉar. aracaṉ, kuṭikaḷ eṉṟa uyarvu tāḻvu aṉpu vāḻttukku ētu? “tirumāl mārpil tirumakaḷpōl eṅkaḷ maṉṉaṉ neñcil iṭi viṭātu uṟiyum pēṟu uṉakku eytuka! nī varakkāṇa yāṅkaḷ muṟpiṟaviyil nalviṉi mikutiyākac ceytiruntōm pōlum. piraccōtaṉaṉuṭiya makaḷ mikac ciṟanta aḻakiyeṉak kēḷviyuṟṟatuṇṭu. anta aḻaki nērilēyē kaṇkuḷirak kāṇac ceyta em aracaṉ vāḻka!” eṉṟu tattiyip palar vāḻttiṉar. anta vāḻttil ‘avaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭaracaṉukku virivil kōpperuntēvi eṉṟa matippukkuriyavaḷ āvāḷ’ eṉṉum perumiyum kalantiruntatu. vēṟu cilar, ivvaḷavu naṟceyalkaḷum piḻi paṭāmal nikaḻvataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātavaṉāy iruntavaṉ yūki eṉpatik kēḷviyuṟṟu, avaṉi vāyāṟa vāḻttiṉar. “naṉṟāka vanta oru poruḷ, ulakil oruvarkkut tītāy muṭintālum, viyappataṟku illi. tītāy vanta oru poruḷ. naṉṟāy muṭintu nalam payattalum, attakiyatu tāṉ. māya yāṉiyāl nam maṉṉaṉi vañcakamākac ciṟi ceyta piraccōtaṉaṉuṭiya tīmi, nam maṉṉaṉukku nalamāy muṭintatōṭu allāmal, aḻakiṟ ciṟanta naṅki tattiyiyum aḷittirukkiṟatu. makaḷik koṭuppōr mutalil tītu pōṟ kāṭṭip piṉpu ceyyumnalam ivvāṟu kuṟippākavē irukkumō?”

ippaṭip palappala eṇṇiṉar cayanti nakarak kuṭi makkaḷ. ittakiya palvakiyāṉa āravāraṅkaḷukkum iṭiyē, amarāpatiyiṟ pukum intiraṉippōlat tatti mutaliyōr piṉtoṭara utayaṇaṉ araṇmaṉiyuḷ pukuntāṉ. utayaṇaṉ cayanti nakarukku vantapiṉ cila nāḷkaḷ amitiyākavum iṉpamākavum kaḻintaṉa. uṟavum cuṟṟamum oṉṟu kūṭiṭak kaḻinta anta nāḷkaḷ, maṟakka muṭiyātaṉa. annāḷkaḷiṭiyē orunāḷ vācavatattiyiṉ tirumaṇa niṉivi am maṇattic ceytu koḷvataṟku uriyavaṉāṉa utayaṇaṉukku naṇparkaḷ uṇṭākkiṉar. ataṉ payaṉāka araṇmaṉik kaṇiyaṉ oru maṅkala nāḷi maṇattiṟku ēṟṟateṉṟu vakuttuk koṭuttāṉ. cōtiṭanūṟ poruḷum tarukka nūṟporuḷum naṉkuaṟintu tērntavaṉ antak kaṇi. araṇmaṉiyil avaṉukkup palavaki mariyātikaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ kūṟiya nāḷi nāṭaṟiya eṭutturikkumāṟu vaḷḷuvarkaḷ āṇi peṟṟaṉar. vēṟṟu nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷukkut takka tūtuvarkaḷ mūlamākat tirumaṇa olikaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. muracuk koṭṭiluḷ teyva vaḻi pāṭṭuṭaṉum arumiyuṭaṉum pēṇappeṟṟu vanta periya periya muracaṅkaḷit tirunāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum paṭi nāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum maṇanāṭ kālaṅkaḷilum yāṉi mēlēṟṟi ūraṟiya olikkac ceytukoṇṭē ceyti kūṟuvatu vaḷḷuvar vaḻakkam. muraci nīrāṭṭic cantaṉam pūci māliyaṇintu yāṉi mēlēṟṟikkoṇṭu utayaṇaṉ tirumaṇa nāḷi aṟivippataṟku vaḷḷuvar pala ticikaḷilum ceṉṟaṉar.

maṉṉaṉ tirumaṇac ceyti kēṭṭa kuṭimakkaḷ nakarip palavakiyālum alaṅkarikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. poṉṉilum muttilum maṇiyilum palvakit tōraṇaṅkaḷ ceytu peru vītikaḷ tōṟum kaṭṭiṉar. vīṭukaḷ, peru vāyil, vītit toṭakkaṅkaḷ, mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilellām vāḻi maramum pākku maramumākak kaṭṭi aḻaku ceytaṉar. nakariluḷḷa aṟac cālikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vantavarkaḷukkellām uṇavaḷikkumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉar. iḷanaṅkiyarkaḷum ēval makaḷirum kuṅkumam mutaliyavaṟṟik kuḻittu aḻakiya vēṟu pala vaṇṇak kuḻampukaḷiyum koṇṭu terukkaḷil kavarcciyāṉa kōlaṅkaḷi iṭṭaṉar. kōvilkaḷilum kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟappāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷ nikaḻttap peṟṟaṉa. palavakiyilum taṅkaḷ aracar tirumaṇa nikaḻcciyiṟ paṅku koṇṭavarpōla nakari aḻakiṟkumēl aḻaku ceytu makiḻntaṉar makkaḷ. tirumaṇa naṉṉāḷ vantatu. nakareṅkum āravāramum makiḻcciyum niṟintu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pala nāṭukaḷiṉ aracarum vantiruntaṉar. tirumaṇa nīrāṭṭuvataṟkāka aḻakiṟ ciṟanta kaṉṉi makaḷir mañcaṉanīr koṇṭuvarap puṟappaṭṭaṉar. cilampukaḷ kālil oli ceyyak koṭi iṭiyil kuṭattunīr calacalakka mañcaṉanīr koṇṭu vantataṟkup piṟaku, avarkaḷ nalla nāḻiki nōkki nīrāṭṭak kāttiruntaṉar. nāḻiki nōkki irunta nāḻikik kaṇakkar, ēṟṟa nēram vantatum kūṟiṉar. tī vēṭṭu maṇam nikaḻttavēṇṭiya poḻutum vantu cērntatu.

muṟippaṭi amikkappaṭṭirunta tirumaṇap pantaril naṭuvē maṟivalāḷar mantiram ota, maṇa vēḷvi ceytāṉ utayaṇaṉ. pakkattilē tatti maṉakkōlattil eḻuti vitta cittirap poṟpāvipōla amarntiruntāḷ. kāmaṉum iratiyum aṭuttaṭuttu amarntiruntatu pōlak kāṇpōrkkut tōṉṟiyatu, ik kāṭci. iṭiyē avaṉ tattiyik kip piṭittu aruntati kāṭṭiya tōṟṟam, kāṇa makiḻcci aḷippatāy iruntatu. aruntati kāṭṭi, vaciṭṭaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu, nāṉmukak kaṭavuḷi vēḷvik kuḻiyil avaṉukkuriya ticiyil vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ utayaṇaṉ. teyva nivētaṉaṅkaḷ ceytavāṟē maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷi aṭuttaṭuttu iyaṟṟiṉāṉ maṇamakaṉākiya avaṉ.

maṇamākāta kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ palar uḻuntu, nel, uppu, malar, veṟṟilic curuḷ, cantaṉam mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷit tattiyiṉ kiyil aḷittu, ēḻu muṟi kaṇavaṉik kikūppi vaṇaṅkumpaṭi vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. kāñcaṉi, tattikkup pakkattil iruntu, avaḷ nāṇam aṭiyā vaṇṇam utayaṇaṉōṭu neruṅki uṭkārumāṟu ceytāḷ. avaḷuṭiya melliya malari otta aṭikaḷit taṉ kiyāl paṟṟi ammimēl vittapōtum, avaḷ pūṅkaraṅkaḷil pori cinti vēḷvikku iṭumāṟu ceytapōtum iṉpa uṇarcciyāl pullarippu ēṟpaṭṭatu utayaṇaṉukku, maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ ellām muṭintapiṉ tattiyiṉ valakkiyip paṟṟikkoṇṭu tī valam vantāṉ utayaṇaṉ. mutiyōrkaḷ nallāci kūṟiṉar. avaḷik kip paṟṟi naṭantapōtu, mella aṭipeyarttu vittatu, ‘tanti, tāy, uṟaviṉaraṟṟa anta iṭattil valiya taṉ kātalaṉi maṇam ceytu koṇṭati eṇṇi, avaḷ taḷarcciyaṭintu viṭṭāḷō?’ eṉṟu utayaṇaṉukkut tōṉṟiyatu. palavitac ciṟappukkaḷuṭaṉ eḻilmikkatāka alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirunta maṇavaṟip paḷḷikkaṭṭilil ēṟumpōtu, iruvarukkumē entavitamāṉa taḷarvumilli. naṉṟāka alaṅkāram ceytu tattiyi utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ aṉuppiya kāñcaṉi, itaḻk kaṭiyil oru kuṟumpup puṉṉakiyi neḷiyaviṭṭuk koṇṭē nakarntāḷ. tali kuṉintavāṟē utayaṇaṉuṭaṉ paḷḷiyaṟiyi nōkki naṭantāḷ vācavatatti. itaṉ piṟaku āṟu tiṅkaḷ makiḻcciyākavum amitiyākavum ceṉṟatu. urumaṇṇuvāviṉ poṟuppil viṭṭuc ceṉṟirunta cayanti nakara āṭci eppōtum pōlak kuṭikaḷ makiḻa āḷappaṭṭu vantatu. utayaṇaṉ aṅkiruntum, appōtutāṉ tirumaṇamākiyirunta avaṉukku āṭcip poṟuppukkaḷic ceytaliṉāl ceyal mikuntu cōrvu tōṉṟum eṉṟu karuti, urumaṇṇuvāvē avvāṭcip poṟuppi mēṟpārttu vantāṉ. yāvum iṉitē nikaḻntu vantaṉa. tirumaṇamāṉa piṉpu āṟāvatu tiṅkaḷil ceyvataṟku uriya uṭaṉ mayir kaḷital eṉṟōr caṭaṅkukku ēṟṟa nallanāḷ vantatu. ciṟappāka ac caṭaṅku nikaḻntu muṭintapiṉ, eṇṇey nīrāṭṭup peṟutal marapu. āyiratteṭṭup poṟkuṭaṅkaḷiṉ mañcaṉa nīr koṇṭuvarap peṟṟatu. utayaṇaṉukku āṭavarum tattikku āyattup peṇṭirum eṇṇey aṇintaṉar. 'maṉiyaṟam ciṟakkumāṟu kaṟpāl māṭci peṟuka’ eṉṟu vāḻttiyavāṟē āyamakaḷir neyyaṇintaṉar.

neyyaṇi nīrāṭṭu mutaliya yāvum muṭinta piṉṉar aṟiñarkaḷākiya periyōr palar, “ic caṭaṅkukaḷellām āṉatum, vaḻakkamākac ceyvataṟkuriya kulateyva vaḻi pāṭṭiyum nī ceytal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu utayaṇaṉukku eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar, teyva vaṇakkattiṟkup piṉṉar nakar valam ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum avarkaḷ utayaṇaṉukku niṉivūṭṭiṉar. utayaṇaṉ avar kūṟṟi maṉamuvantu ēṟṟukkoṇṭu avvāṟu ceyya muṉvantāṉ. ciṉālayattil kōvil koṇṭirukkum ciṉatēvaṉākiya arukak kaṭavuḷit tattiyōṭu ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. teyva vaṇakkattiṟkuppiṉ iruvarum maṇakkālattuk koṇṭa atē maṇak kōlam pūṇṭu araca ciṉṉaṅkaḷ puṭicūḻap peruñciṟappuṭaṉ nakarvalañ ceytaṉar. māṭaṅkaḷilum māḷikikaḷilum āvaṇamaṟukukaḷilum cantikaḷilum nakar valaṅ kāṇpataṟkup palar kūṭiyiruntaṉar. tattiyi maṇak kōlattōṭu pārkka vīṭukaḷiṉ mēl māṭaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ palarkūṭi niṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉar. nakaram muḻuvatum taṅkaḷ maṉṉaṉ maṇakkōlattil valam varuvatik kaṇṭu makiḻccik kaṭaluḷ āḻntatu. nakarvalam muṭinta piṉṉum vaḻakkamākac ceyyum eṇṇey nīrāṭṭukkuppiṉ iruvarum maṇaviṉpattiṉ nukarcciyil tiḷittaṉar. nāḷ ellām uvaki niṟiyac ceṉṟaṉa. uvaki yellām ulakil avarkaḷiruvarukkē urimi yāyiṟṟu.





23. yūkiyiṉ payaṇam

iṅkē cayanti nakaril utayaṇaṉ vācavatatti mutaliyōr ivvāṟu makiḻcciyōṭu irukkum vēḷiyil ivarkaḷip piṭiyēṟṟi aṉuppiya piṟaku aṅkē uñci nakaril yūki eṉṉa ceytāṉ eṉpatik kāṇpōm. utayaṇaṉip piṭiyil ēṟṟi aṉuppiya piṉpu yūki, cātakaṉ eṉṉum kuyavaṉiṉ kuṭilil cāṅkiyat tāyic cantittu mēlē nikaḻtta vēṇṭiya cila tiṭṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟic cintittāṉ eṉpatu muṉpē kūṟappaṭṭutu. utayaṇaṉ nalamāka vācavatattiyuṭaṉ nakar aṭintiruppāṉ eṉpatu teriyiṉum, iṅkē uñci nakaril taṉṉiyum utayaṇaṉiyum taṉṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiya maṟṟavarkaḷiyum paṟṟit tavaṟāka eṇṇik koḷḷātapaṭi cila poyc ceytikaḷip parappiviṭṭup piṉputāṉ nakar tirumpa vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉāṉ yūki. maliyil ōriṭattil oru marak kiḷiyil viḷinta tēṉum nāṭṭil oriṭattil oru pacuviṉiṭam kaṟanta pālum vēṟuvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil tōṉṟiya poruḷkaḷtām. āṉāl avi oṉṟupaṭṭālum pirintiruntālum orē iṉcuvi uṭiyaviyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. yūkiyip poṟuttavariyil taṉakkum utayaṇaṉukkum uḷḷa naṭpi, attakiyatākavē eṇṇiyiruntāṉ. araciyal toṭarpuḷḷa ceyalkaḷilum cūḻcciyōṭu kūṭiyavaṟṟi māṟupaṭāmal ceytu muṭippatilum muṟṟit tērnta yūki, naṭpuṇarcciyil ivvaḷavu arumiyāṉa piṇippu uṭiyavaṉ.

pāḻpaṭṭu iṭintu, cila pakutikaḷ maṭṭumē eñci yirunta mākāḷa vaṉattuk kāḷi kōyilit taṉakkum taṉṉuṭaṉ vantavarkaḷukkum maṟivāṉa vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭirunta yūki, māṟuvēṭaṅ koṇṭu uñci nakarak kōṭṭi vāyilukkuḷ ceṉṟu nakaril pukuntāṉ. aṅkē paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil palarum aṟiyumpaṭiyākat tāṉ kūṟak karutiyirunta poyc ceytikaḷip parappiṉāṉ. “piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ, uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu pūṇṭu utayaṇaṉit taṉ makaḷ tattikku yāḻ kaṟpikkac ceytāṉ. putalvarkaḷukkum avaṉikkoṇṭu paṭikkalap payiṟci aḷittāṉ. utayaṇaṉi virumpi avaṉukkut taṉ makaḷi maṉamāṟa aḷittē piraccōtaṉa maṉṉaṉ iruvariyum piṭiyēṟṟi aṉuppiṉāṉ. tattiyum utayaṇaṉmēl mikka kātalkoṇṭē avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉaḷ. piraccōtaṉaṉ vekuṇṭeḻuntu paṭiyōṭu utayaṇaṉip piṉpaṟṟātiruttalē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu” eṉṟu yūki ēṟṟa kāraṇattōṭu iṇiya ceytikaḷip palarum aṟiyap potuiṭaṅkaḷil eṭuttu urittāṉ.

‘piraccōtaṉaṉ, cālaṅkāyaṉuṭiya aṟivuriyālum taṉ maṉaamitiyālum utayaṇaṉip paṟṟi avvaḷavākac ciṉaṅ koṇṭirukkamāṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟa muṭivu yūkikku appōtu naṉṟākat terintiruntatu. ākavētāṉ intap poyc ceytikaḷic cila ciṟiya uṇmik kalappuṭaṉ muṟṟum meyyēpōlap parappuvatil avaṉukkut taṭikaḷ eviyum ēṟpaṭavilli. potu maṉṟaṅkaḷ, kōyil muṉṟilkaḷ, yāṉiccērikaḷ, paṭiñarvītikaḷ, perunterukkaḷ eṅkum evarum aṟiyap paraviviṭṭatu yūkiyiṉ ceyti, aṅkaṅkē aṟinta palar, aṟiyāta cilarukkut tāmē eṭutturittaṉar. uñci nakaril yūki ceyya vēṇṭiya vēli anēkamāka niṟivēṟiyatu. aṉṟiravu mākāḷa vaṉattuk kāḷikōyil atuvari kāṇāta palarik kaṇṭatu.

uñci nakarukku appāl veḷiyē amintuḷḷa payaṅkaramāṉa antak kāḷikōyiliṉ citintupōṉa cuvarkaḷukku naṭuvē palar kūṭiyiruntaṉar. iṭintupōṉa cuvar oṉṟiṉmītu periya tīvaṭṭi oṉṟu erintu koṇṭiruntatu. yūki avarkaḷukku muṉ niṉṟu maṉam urukum vakiyil ētō pēcik koṇṭiruntāṉ. tīvaṭṭiyiṉ maṅkalāṉa oḷiyil, kōviliṉ citiyāmalirunta uṭpuṟattil kāḷitēviyiṉ payaṅkaramāṉa periya cili terintu koṇṭiruntatu. ēṟakkuṟiyak kaṟpaṉi uyaram amintirunta pirammāṇṭamāṉa antak kāḷitēviyiṉ cilikku muṉṉāl niṉṟutāṉ yūki pēcik koṇṭiruntāṉ. kūṭiyiruntavarkaḷ atuvari uñci nakaril avaṉ ceyta pala cūḻccimikka ceyalkaḷiṉ veṟṟikku uṟutuṇiyāka irunta māṟuvēṭattōṭu kūṭiya avaṉ maṉitarkaḷ. utayaṇaṉ, piraccōtaṉaṉāṟ ciṟippaṭṭatu kēṭṭu, tāṉ iṟantatākap poyyip parappiviṭṭu uñcikku māṟu vēṭattil yūki vantapōtu avaṉuṭaṉ vantavarkaḷtām attaṉi pērum.
